**¶2** [And likewise concerning] the darkness, the world [of] their root [is] which poured forth [from below,] the one that the earth has [in it, the great] darkness [the] it is far from the light [and]

**¶3** [*] This is the first [great lesson] concerning the eternality; he [has spoken] to you, for each one of them exists [eternally,] they are divided from one another by their [own boundaries,] in an eternality since the [scripture] which I have written for you; this is, that [when] the darkness has made war against the light, desiring that it would reign over an essence not its own. Conversely, the darkness which is in [it is cast] out, and it is gathered in to a bond [and the seals] unveil [the powers of the light] and they go in to him and become [as a pe]rfume [r]et up [st]d they reign for ever, and a single God comes to be [ov]er the totality, being above the totality. You find no opponent from this time on against the Father, the King of the Light and the [Holy Spirit.]

**¶9** [These are the natures] which they occur in, from the beginning [until the separation,] they are mixed and joined with one another.

**¶12** (6) The first great lesson [is this,] which in the [twelve signs] that walks in it because of the twelve [months] in it, which in the cities and the dwelling places found in the [world] because of the doors that are opened in them [heavens] upon the earth, which in the four corners [these are the measures of the universe] and my pains. For also [are my labours] and my afflictions [my toils] to them [and the knowledge] suffices for the person [to become] wise person.

> **Structural findings:**
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶1–¶3: The chapter opens with a binary eternal distinction (light/darkness) and immediately frames the conflict as a disordering will from below seeking rule over what is not its own. This coheres with discr
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶4–¶7: The sequence of agents (First Man → Living Spirit → Third Ambassador) is a stable Manichaean triad and maps well to degree-functions, but the text’s phrasing blurs their functional distinctness in pla
> - [seam_artifact] ¶¶6: The phrase “and also above he [three]e earths” is introduced with a bridge connective and yields an awkward numeric claim (“three earths”) that does not function as a discrete-degree triad here; it re
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶5 and ¶12: The repeated TWELVE (twelve signs / twelve months) functions as a complete system of ordered truths in ultimates (cosmic measures/cycles) and is structurally coherent, not an expansion beyond the thre
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶8: The eschatological closure stacks reversals and final unities (“fire blazes,” “devoured,” “purifier,” “single God”) with several unclear pronominal subjects (“he/it/they”). This is not a degree violat

**Spiritual Assessment:** This introduction is a compressed cosmogony whose genuine substrate is triadic and influx-ordered: (1) two eternal realms distinguished by boundary (light as the celestial source-state; darkness as the opposed self-derived will), (2) a descent of salvific operation into combat and order (spiritual degree: truth-forming, separating, structuring), and (3) an ultimate cosmic arrangement in effects (natural degree: world-structure as containment, measures, cycles, and uses). The chapter’s spiritual burden is not “matter as evil,” but the necessity of an ultimate fixed structure (“bond,” “foundation,” cycles, signs, measures) that both contains disorder and provides a stage for purification/separation. Several places show editorial seam language (“and also”) and a likely numeric intrusion (“three earths”) that does not map cleanly onto the three discrete degrees in the way the rest of the chapter does. The most structurally sound number here is TWELVE (complete system of truths in ultimates: signs/months/order), while “four corners/mountains” reads as natural completeness (world-extensions/quarters) rather than a degree-taxonomy.

**Summary:** Findings: 5 (2 confirmed structures, 2 coherence issues, 1 seam artifact). Changes made in 6 of 10 paragraphs, mainly (a) smoothing a likely expansion seam in ¶6 (“and also … three earths”) by bracketing it as uncertain expansion, and (b) clarifying triadic agent-functions (First Man / Living Spirit / Third Ambassador) without replacing Kephalaia names. No pentadic taxonomies appeared; TWELVE and FOUR function coherently as natural-degree completeness/measures rather than editorial overgrowth. Confidence: medium-high on the triadic agent structure and the TWELVE correspondences; medium on the “three earths” expansion judgment due to lacunose context.

## Chapter 1: Concerning the Ad[vent] of the Apostle.

**¶3** Understand, O my beloved ones: All the apostles who are on occasion sent to the world resemble farmers; while their churches, which they choose, are like Parmuthi and Paophi. For, the way Parmuthi occurs not in all the months of the year; nor does Paophi [also occur] in all of them. Rather, the season [is appointed] for it alone, as they know [the order of] its vegetables [and the growth of] the time they are cropped; also the vegetables and the [of each] season; and they tend and ripen the pickings, and they [carry them up] to Paophi.

**¶4** Of course, now that farmer ⟨p.10⟩ [does not sow in] Parmuthi; in that, if he will come and sow some corn seed there [it will not become] cereal. And he shall be involved from the beginning [and receive] a corn seed and toil for it. Yet, when the summer comes each year, and his corn comes to the season of its harvest; then the farmer comes forth and harvests it. Even the gardener from the beginning [the gardener too,] shall toil for his fruits; and he tends them and he [waters them] and they ripen; and he comes forth and picks them from the tree.

**¶6** [*] ⟨p.11⟩ The way [an apostle is sent] many years before [he comes to] the world: as he journeys below to the [depths,] he makes the form of his church free, and [loosens the bonds] of the flesh, whose forms he had made free [from the bond].

**¶7** Like the farmer, for when he will [sow,] that moment he shall begin [to] tend [it until] it reaches Parmuthi also, he may harvest it [and not] toil for the fruits of Paophi [and not].

**¶8** Again, this too is [how] the apostles [are sent] from the beginning of the moulding of humanity [in the heights], as I have told you, that when they [will appear] before every thing he shall [make its form] free above, first.

**¶9** [*] Yet, when it [will be] born in the flesh, he shall come down to it and at once [release] it in the [bond] which comes out from the [flesh; and it] is raised up to the greatness. He shall continue in the world at this time [until they will know] him, corresponding to the season when the world will come to his [measure,] when the season will mature, he is raised up from the world, and he leaves his church {behind} and goes forth. Still, he [is not far from it,] and he helps and aids his church. Whoever comes out from their body after him, he shall become for them a support and a leader; and he goes on before them [in the way,], excepting the others who are set firmly in the flesh. He is the one who will become for them a helper and a guardian; he shall not take [part in its labour] in secret, until his church [shall attain] change it [to the other state,] when his church [has finished,] he was raised up after it, and went into the land of glory. And he rejoices and rests at the last [from the labour.]

**¶10** Like the farmer who shall be glad of heart in [the day when] he was bringing in (the harvest). Or like the merchant who shall come up from a country with the doubling of his great cargo; and the riches of his trading.

**¶11** When the apostle will be raised up from the world, he and his church [rejoice,] for every apostle [labours] in the [world.] Since [a fruit] tree, that still is laden with its first fruits [when they are ripe;], and they are picked; they shall propagate other ones there, and they mature to the season to bring them forth. There is not any time the tree is bare of fruit! Rather, instead, those about to ripen as they pick them shall propagate other ones there. When they bear these [and set] them on the tree; they come up and are brought forth upon its branches. They shall pick from it those that mature and ripen. The farmer does not rest at any time from the labour and the pain. At what hour may he cease? When he can finish harvesting the corn in Parmuthi! [and set] them in the winter, the fruits of the summer shall [So also] grow up and be picked in the month of Paophi.

**¶12** ⟨p.12⟩ The apostles are like this also. Now, when the apostle will be raised up to the heights, he and his church, and they depart from the world; at that instant another apostle shall be sent to it, to another church [which he shall make free] it [in the heights,]. Yet, first, he shall make the forms of his church free in the heights, as I have told you. When [it will be born in the flesh]; again, he too shall come down and appear [in the world;] and he releases his church and saves it from the flesh [of sin ...]

**¶20** ⟨p.14⟩ Like a tree will be plucked, and the fruits on it taken away. And it remains behind without fruit.

**¶23** Yet, when the church assumed the flesh, the season arrived to redeem the souls; like the month of Parmuthi that cereal shall ripen in, to be harvested.

**¶24** [*] He unveiled to me the hidden mystery, the one that is hidden from the worlds and the generations: the mystery of the depths and the heights. He unveiled to me the mystery of the light and the darkness: how the conflict arose and how it was resisted. Afterwards, he unveiled to me how this universe was set up as a bound order through mingling, so that separation and restoration might be accomplished in ultimates.

⟨EXPANSION: “the calamity of conflict, and the war, and the great [onslaught,], the battle that the darkness spread about”; “the way that the ships were constructed; to enable the gods of light to be in them, to purify the light from creation. Conversely, the dregs and the effluent [are cast down] to the abyss”; “the mystery of the fashioning of Adam, the first man”; “the mystery of the tree of knowledge, which Adam ate from”⟩

**¶25** [*] [Merged into ¶24 in harmonization.]

> **Structural findings:**
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶3-4: The farmer/month allegory is a genuine correspondential structure about ordered seasons (states) and the necessity of timing in spiritual work (sowing, tending, harvest). It is not a degree-taxonomy; 
> - [seam_artifact] ¶¶6: Bridge connective “Also” introduces a new explanatory layer and the sentence is fractured; it reads like a stitched insertion. The core meaning is coherent (preparatory descent/loosening), but the sea
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶8-9: The sequence ‘make free above first → born in flesh → come down/release → raised up → remains as helper/guardian’ is a clean influx model and maps well to discrete degrees without needing extra entiti
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶9: A few phrases appear to be editorial padding or damaged repetition (“and and”; “he shall not take [part in its labour] in secret”), but they do not introduce new structural items. Minimal smoothing is
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶11-12: The ‘tree never bare’ and ‘as one apostle rises another is sent’ expresses continuous providential succession (no gap in influx). This is structurally consistent and not a pentadic expansion.
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶20, ¶23: These are isolated similes (‘tree plucked’ / ‘Parmuthi harvest’) with missing connective tissue due to lacunae. They likely belonged to a continuous argument about churches that fail vs churches harve
> - [pentadic_expansion] ¶¶24-25: The ‘mystery’ catalogue is additive and drifts into cosmological mechanics (light/darkness, war, mingling, ships, purification, dregs, Adam, tree). It does not present a triadic descent through degree

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter is a structural description of how Divine influx provides help to the world by periodic “advents” of an apostolic function. In correspondence, the “apostle” is not primarily a historical office but the descending operation of higher degree into lower: first ordering in the heights (celestial/spiritual preparation), then appearing in the world (natural effect/use), then remaining present as interior support after bodily departure (continued spiritual influx to the church). The farming-month allegory (seasons that do not occur “in all months”) teaches that revelation/renewal is not arbitrary but timed to states: sowing (implantation of good), growth (formation of truth), harvest (use/ultimates). The later ‘mysteries’ paragraph shifts from this triadic advent-architecture into Mani’s characteristic cosmological inventory (light/darkness, ships, purification machinery, Adam, tree). Much of that inventory is not arranged in discrete-degree order; it reads as an additive catalogue (a “five-ish” sufficiency list) and includes a material-prison/purification mechanism that tends toward the removed Swedenborgian ‘limbus’ problem (light-substance trapped in matter). The substrate structure is strongest where the sequence is: prepare-above → descend/appear → raise up/continue-help; it weakens where it becomes a named/technical mythos of cosmological mechanics without degree-mapping.

**Summary:** Findings: 7 total (4 confirmed structures, 2 seam/coherence smoothings, 1 major expansion reduction). Changes made: removed one bridge connective (¶6), lightly smoothed a duplicated seam (¶9), and reduced the late ‘mystery’ catalogue (¶24-25) to a triadic substrate disclosure with the mechanical/mythic inventory retained as ⟨EXPANSION⟩. Dominant issue: additive cosmological inventory that does not map to discrete degrees and introduces matter-as-extraction mechanics. Confidence: high on the apostolic advent/farming structure (¶3-12); medium-high that ¶24-25 is an editorial expansion; low-medium on ¶20/¶23 due to fragmentary context.

## Chapter 2: The Second, concerning the Parable of the Tree.

**¶5** [*] Great is the error of those who speak by their own mouth, as though the sects were one thing. For what they explain about the two trees they make many and various, and they do not grasp the explanation of the tree. For the true tree, as it grows on this composite earth—like the date palm—has portions that are taken and portions that are cast away, for not absolutely every part of the date palm is used.

**¶6** [*] They liken it wrongly and give a decision about the tree as though it were wholly sweet. Yet sweetness is found in its fibres and branches, and there is also dust and refuse that clings in its bark, and parts that are cast away. Therefore their judgment fails when they establish their writing against mankind and name a tree as though it were wholly one thing.

**¶11** [Know then] how I will reveal to you [plainly] the expound[er] the trees [of the teaching] the Father [concerning] all of them, as he [of Light is above] in the [who made] world [upper] this good tree, the [of Light;] the light of this good tree [rooted in the aeons;] the Pillar [its radiance is]

**¶12** ⟨p.20⟩ ... the intellectuals that are cloaked upon its body [and they are called] this good tree. They are the light elements that are mingled, being compounded in the totality.

**¶14** Behold, this is the good tree [and its fruit is Life]

**¶15** [*] [Again] I reveal to you: the good tree is known by its threefold order—its inmost source, its ordering form, and its ultimate fruit. Its source is the Father who dwells in greatness, perfect in the aeons of light. Its ordering power is the Pillar of Glory ⟨= the Perfect Man⟩, by which the light is held in form. Its ultimate operation in the totality is manifest in the living powers that bear it into effect ⟨e.g., the First Man in the ship of living waters; the Third Ambassador in the ship of living fire⟩.

⟨EXPANSION: “it has five limbs… consideration, counsel, insight, thought, mind… consideration is the holy church… counsel is the Pillar… insight is the First Man… thought is the Third Ambassador… mind is the Father.”⟩

**¶17** [*] By this order those who belong to the light are gathered and lifted: from the ultimate reception toward the ordering form, and from the ordering form toward the inmost source.

⟨EXPANSION: the five-step ascent ladder (“raised up to counsel… to insight… to thought… bring them in to mind”).⟩

**¶18** This is the good tree that shall give good fruit, [whose fruit] is the entire life and light for ever [which] occurs [in the aeons] ever, it is blessed [for ever and]

**¶19** [*] ⟨p.21⟩ [Again concerning the] bad [tree:] it is from the composite world in contrast to the good tree—like smoke and the fire of furnaces. Its ruling power is the order of error that Satan appointed in the world, and its fruit is what walks in aching and error.

⟨EXPANSION: “five rulers… heaven and the wheel of the sphere… five fleshes…”⟩

**¶20** The taste of these bad fruits is in evil people and the sects, who are bound in law after law—the law of death. Taking taste for it and thirsting for it, they become souls of death.

**¶22** [*] I reveal to you the bad tree by its threefold order—its inmost ruling love, its ordering falsity, and its ultimate bondage. Its source is matter and darkness as the love of self and domination. Its ordering form is the law of death by which they take instruction. Its ultimate effect is bondage in the depths, without repentance while that state is loved.

⟨EXPANSION: the five-limb list (“consideration, counsel, insight, thought, mind… transmigration… furnaces… vessel… lump, last bond…”).⟩

**¶23** [Behold,] this is the explanation of the bad tree, as I have explained to you, which you [have listened] to it [and have not] good fruit [joined] its fruit bad [but] in it, in its existent days [and has not ceased] in it.

**¶24** [*] Concerning this good tree, my beloved ones, I say to you in truth: it is in its good earth—life and light—and death and darkness do not exist within it. The bad tree is in its bad earth: darkness and death.

⟨ANNOTATION: “the bad tree is Matter” is read correspondentially as the natural plane turned to darkness (ultimate receptacle perverted), not as physical matter being evil in itself.⟩

**¶25** ⟨p.23⟩ Blessed is every one [who understands] these two trees, and separates them one from another. He understands that they did not arise out of one another, nor did they come from one another. They did not come from one! The person who can separate them out may go up to the aeon of light, [as I have] revealed it to you.

**¶26** And he sees [that the] bad fruit did not arise from it [good tree’s]; but whoever will not separate out [from the two roots] from one another, he falls to the land of darkness [in this mixture] until he arrives at the [lump.]

**¶27** Blessed is whoever can believe! He [separates Light from] darkness; which grows out of the [bad earth] for ever and ever.

> **Structural findings:**
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶5–¶6: The opening uses the date palm to refute simplistic ‘two-tree’ talk, but the paragraph is textually jagged and includes contradictory phrasing (“bad [good and]”). The underlying argument is coherent: 
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶11–¶14: The identification of the good tree with the Father of Light above, rooted in the aeons, and associated with a “Pillar” is structurally consonant: root/source (inmost), ordered stability/axis (form), 
> - [pentadic_expansion] ¶¶15–¶17 (good tree): A five-limb taxonomy (“consideration, counsel, insight, thought, mind”) is imposed and then mapped to ecclesial/cosmic officials (holy church, Pillar/Perfect Man, First Man, Third Ambassador, Father).
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶18: The conclusion of the good-tree section identifies fruit with “entire life and light for ever,” a clean ultimate-effect statement.
> - [pentadic_expansion] ¶¶19–¶22 (bad tree): The bad tree is likewise forced into a fivefold scheme: “five rulers… five fleshes…” and then “five limbs” again with a punitive ladder (law of death, transmigration, furnaces, vessel, lump/bond). Thi
> - [degree_violation] ¶¶24: “the bad tree is Matter… darkness and death” risks treating matter as ontologically evil (a metaphysical source of evil), which conflicts with the corrected correspondential principle that the natural
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶25–¶27: The closing blessing centers on discernment/separation: separating the two trees, not confusing their roots, yields ascent to light; failure yields descent into darkness. This is a coherent moral-arch

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter uses the “two trees” as a correspondential map of two ruling loves (good vs. evil) as they descend into forms and produce fruit (effects). In genuine spiritual architecture, a tree is a full emanation image: root (inmost source/love), trunk-branches (ordering truth/form), fruit (ultimate use/effect). The chapter’s strongest substrate is the functional contrast: a good tree whose fruit is life/light (order, ascent, integration) versus a bad tree whose fruit is death/darkness (disorder, bondage, descent). However, the chapter is heavily overlaid by a **fivefold limb** taxonomy on both trees. That pentadic scheme does not map onto the three discrete degrees and shows classic Mani-institutional features: (a) a “church” limb, (b) a ladder-like ascent through five stations, and (c) mirrored fivefold condemnation (“law… transmigration… furnaces… vessel… lump/bond”). The substrate beneath it is triadic: (1) the inmost source (Father of Light vs. darkness/matter as ruling love), (2) the mediating form/order (the ordering power that arranges and separates), (3) the ultimate effect/fruit (life/light vs. death/darkness). The chapter’s insistence that the two roots do not arise from one another is doctrinally dualist; structurally, it functions as a moral-spiritual discriminant (separation of loves) more than a genuine emanation law of reality, and it sits in tension with the correspondential principle that all lower forms are recipients/containers rather than independent sources.

**Summary:** Findings: 7 total (3 confirmed structures, 2 pentadic expansions reduced, 1 coherence seam smoothed in the damaged opening, 1 matter-as-evil degree risk annotated). Changes made to ¶5–¶6 (smoothing), ¶15–¶17 (reduced to triadic substrate; fivefold ladder annotated as expansion), ¶19 and ¶22 (reduced; fivefold rulers/limbs annotated), and ¶24 (annotated to prevent ontological-evil reading of matter). Dominant issue: mirrored fivefold ‘limbs’ taxonomy overriding an underlying triadic tree architecture. Confidence: high on identifying the pentadic lists as expansion; medium on the exact best triadic mapping of named powers (First Man / Ambassador) because the text is partially lacunose and may preserve authentic functional distinctions even while being pressed into a fivefold ladder.

**¶5** [happiness]ess of the glorious one is the Father, the God of [truth, who] is established in the great land of [light]. ⟨p.24⟩ His glorious wis[d]om is his Great Spirit that [is sent forth from him into the deep] below, which flows through all his aeons, and t[h]ey float therein. His great power is all the gods, the rich ones and the angels who were summoned from him as they [came forth] t]hey that are called aeons [and these are]

The] glorious [happiness] is the [Living] Spirit ⟨function: celestial proceeding—love as active descent⟩, [and the wisdom] is the Mother of Lif[ife ⟨function: spiritual ordering/forming⟩; and great power] is [all] the gods, [the] rich o]nes and the angels ⟨function: natural administrations/uses in ultimates⟩ who are within the ship.

**¶7** [*] Again, [h]appiness, wisdom and power exist in the s[ship of liv]ing waters [and they are ordered in it as in a dwelling] the happiness [is the Living Soul,] the Mind of the Father ⟨function: celestial good present in a lower vessel⟩. Wisdom is the [five sons of the] Living Spirit; and great powe[r is gathered from the fi]ve sons of the First Man [ve lights, who are encl]osed and compounded in the totality, that [is held together] while he supports the totality.

> **Structural findings:**
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶4: The chapter announces a triad (happiness, wisdom, power) and explicitly links each to a ‘place’ (Light/Truth/Power), which corresponds cleanly to the three discrete degrees.
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶5: The triad is instantiated at the highest scale: Father (happiness), Great Spirit sent forth (wisdom), and the summoned host (power). This is a proper descent: source → ordering → operative effect.
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶6: Textual duplication/seam noise appears: ‘which is called the sun’ is repeated and the line ‘the second greatne[dor, the second greatness’ is garbled. This looks like transmission noise rather than Man
> - [seam_artifact] ¶¶7: The paragraph begins with ‘Again’ and then ‘Also,’ marking iterative enumeration. Here they function as list-headers rather than a 3→4 expansion seam; still, ‘Also’ is redundant inside the triad and c
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶6–¶7: Across ‘sun/ship of living fire’ and ‘ship of living waters,’ the same triad recurs with different names. This is naming-variation over a stable functional substrate, not additional entities.
> - [degree_violation] ¶¶8 (clause: ‘Wisdom is the five sons...’; ‘power ... from the five sons...’): At first glance ‘five sons’ could be read as a pentadic intrusion. However, it does not occupy positions 4–5 in the triad; it sits inside the wisdom/power slots as a subordinate set. The chapter does 

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter is a correspondential lesson on how a single triadic reality (celestial/spiritual/natural) appears at multiple scales. The triad is named “happiness / wisdom / power,” but the functions are clear: (1) an inmost state of blessed love/rest (celestial; the ‘place of Light’), (2) an ordering/forming principle (spiritual; the ‘place of Truth’), and (3) a host of operative forces in ultimates (natural; the ‘place of Power’), described as “gods, rich ones, angels.” The text then reiterates the same triad across descending containments—Father’s own realm, the sun/ship of living fire, the ship of living waters, and the elements—showing influx: what is one in the inmost is differentiated as functions in lower degrees. The main structural tension is the introduction of “five sons” twice in ¶8: these do not present as a 4th/5th-item expansion to a triad, but as a subordinate quantitative set (five = sufficiency/‘much’) inside the spiritual and natural functions. The chapter’s genuine architecture is therefore a repeated triad across vessels, not a pentadic taxonomy.

**Summary:** Findings: 6 total (3 confirmed-structure, 2 seam/coherence repairs, 1 flagged-but-retained ‘five’ as subordinate quantity). Changes made: removed obvious duplication/garble in ¶6; removed redundant ‘Also’ in ¶7; added minimal functional annotations where naming overlays could obscure the triadic degree-positions. Dominant issue: transmission/seam noise rather than Mani’s pentadic expansion. Confidence: high that the chapter’s core is a genuine triadic substrate; moderate on the exact wording of ¶6 due to corruption.

## Chapter 4: Concerning the Four Great Days that have come forth from one another, together with the Four Nights

**¶2** The first great day is the Father, the God of truth, the fir[st-born, who dwells a]idst the aeons of his greatness, in his [l]iving kingdom. The twelve hours of this great day are the twelve great rich Gods of greatness. These, who are the first evocations that he evoked (to mirror) his greatness, he spread out to the four climes, three by three before his face.

**¶3** The second day is the Third Ambassador, the one who dwells in the light ship. His twelve hours are the twelve virgins that he evoked in his greatness.

**¶4** [*] [The third day is the Pillar of Glory, the great porter that is greater than] all [po]rters; the one that supports all [the universe], those above and those below. Its twelve hours are the five sons of the First Man and the five sons of the Living Spirit, who support all the weights of the universe. ⟨EXPANSION: “together with the summons and the obedience, which are counted to their ten brethren.”⟩ These are the light hours of the third day.

**¶5** [*] ⟨EXPANSION / NAMING OVERLAY: The awakener of light ⟨= Jesus the Splendour⟩, localized ‘in his church’; his ‘twelve wisdoms’ as a complete teaching-system. This functions as instruction/awakening within the cosmos, not as a fourth discrete degree beyond the ultimate Pillar.⟩

**¶7** ⟨p.26⟩ [This is the reckoning of the days and nights]: For just as four day[s ex]ist, s[o] too four nights exist.

**¶8** The first night is the land of Darkness. It has twelve black [shadows] therein, which are its dark h[o]ur[rs]. The twelve shadows of the first night are the five elements of the land of Darkness, [which] poured forth from its five sense organs; and [the five kinds of smoke] which [are mingled and hidden] in its [fi]ve elements and its five [vessels]; the five [sp]irits that dwell in its elements [also]. These are the twelve shadows and spirits of the first night.

**¶9** The second night is Matter, the sculptress [of the five worlds] who has sculpted [the forms of males and females] and the entire ruling-power that is in the worl[ds of darkness]. She has fashioned it in five sense organs, five [male] and five female, two per world; together with the fire and the lust [that] dwell in men and women, inflaming [them] inward to one another. These are the twelve spirits of this second night.

**¶10** Indeed she, this Matter, the thought of death, ga[ve] the King of the realms of Darkness and his powers strength for the war and the fight, against the aeons of greatness. Yet, he was caught! They laid a snare for him through the Fi[rst M]an. [He] was ta[ken] t[the heights] through the powers [and the bonds were loosened from him.] was brought up from the land of death and s[et fastened,]ove and below in the [whole] universe; in the five par[t]s in the heavens above, as well as the five [parts] in the earths below; together with the heat and the cold that are the father and the mother, their fire and their l[u]st.

**¶11** This is the second night that is begotten from the first night. They laid a snare for her through the First M[a]n. She was brought up by the hand of the Living Spirit, and set in this composite univers[e; and they balanced] he[r], above and below. The [upper side is the sense] organs of males; the lower side [is the sense organs of] fe[mal]es.

**¶12** ⟨p.27⟩ The thir[d night is the five] worlds of flesh [... five] male, and five female; that were begotten from the powers [abo]ve. They have fallen upon the earth, being displayed in wh[at is d]ry and what is moist; together with the fire and the lust [that d]we[l]l in them, which drives them inward to one another.

**¶13** [*] [Mat]te[r] herself, the thought of death, is the [mother] of them all; as among them she is named 'the night'. [The worlds] of male and female fleshes, together with the fire and the l[ust], shall [make up] twelve hours of [this third] night. The third night that is in the worlds of fl[e]sh was begotten from the second night; which the Living [Spir]it crucified in the uni[verse, and fixed]ove and below in heaven [in the five heights and the five depths].

**¶14** The fourth night is the law of Sin, which is the dark spirit who speaks in the twelve spirits, the twelve sects. They are the nakedne[ss]es, the twelve zodiacal signs of Matter. They are her thrones; she who is made public, as she sculpts and is displayed in the old man.

**¶15** [*] The hours of this fourth night, the old man who reigns in the sects, are the twelve e[vi]l spirits; the [twelve measures] of the old man, which [are the boundaries that]fferentiate the day and night [in the realm] death.

**¶16** This is the fourth night [—four nights] that have come forth of one another. One has [its image in the next] just like the days of lig[ht; and] they manifested one another. So als[o in the darkness] they did arise out of one another.

> **Structural findings:**
> - [coherence_issue] ¶Chapter title / ¶2–¶5: The chapter is titled “four great days,” but the described ‘days’ do not form a coherent discrete-degree sequence. The first three align to the three degrees (Father / Ambassador / Pillar), while the 
> - [pentadic_expansion] ¶¶4: The ‘twelve hours’ of the third day are forced by adding “summons and obedience” to an already complete ten (five sons + five sons). This is a classic institutional expansion to reach the required twe
> - [seam_artifact] ¶¶15: Bridge connective “And, also,” marks a secondary addition and reads like an editorial suture at the transition into a second description of the same ‘twelve hours.’
> - [seam_artifact] ¶¶13: “Again,” functions as a stitching marker introducing a repeated/secondary clause about the third night’s begetting and crucifixion/fixing above and below.
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶2–¶3: Day 1 (Father) with twelve hours (twelve gods) spread to four climes ‘three by three’; Day 2 (Third Ambassador) with twelve virgins. These are coherent uses of twelve (complete system of truths) and ‘
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶8–¶14: The night-hours use many ‘five’ sets (elements, sense organs, worlds). These function as quantity/variety within disorder rather than a supposed degree-structure, and the overall sequence of four nigh

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter is built as a cosmological “calendar”: four days of Light and four nights of Darkness, each with “twelve hours.” Structurally, the reliable substrate is the use of TWELVE to signify a complete system of truths (hours as fixed measures/states), and the mirroring principle (day/night) as the inversion of order (influx from love→wisdom→use versus inversion into selfhood/matter-forming). The main architectural problem is that the “four great days” do not map cleanly onto the three discrete degrees. The first two “days” (Father; Third Ambassador) behave like degree-1 and degree-2 functions (source-love; mediated ordering/truth), while the third day (Pillar of Glory) fits degree-3 (ultimate container/support). The fourth day (Jesus the Splendour in the church) reads as a naming/institutional overlay: an awakener/teacher function inserted as an additional “day,” plus an ecclesial localization (“in his church”) that does not belong to the cosmic degree-sequence. On the “hours” of the third day, the text shows a clear pentadic expansion seam: “ten brethren” (five sons + five sons) is then forced to become twelve by adding “summons and obedience,” which matches the known Mani signature (institutional call-response mechanism). The night-sequence, although heavily “five”-structured (five elements, five sense organs, etc.), is not a degree-taxonomy; it is a quantity-logic description of sufficiency/variety in disorder, so it should mostly be retained. However, the text contains seam connectives (“And, also,” “Again,” “Indeed”) and retroactive sutures (“have come forth of one another”) that function editorially to glue expansions; these are smoothed where they appear at list-joints.

Correspondentially: Day 1 = inmost Divine Love/source (Father). Day 2 = ordering/mediating wisdom that sends forms (Ambassador). Day 3 = ultimate boundary/container (Pillar/Porter) in which the universe is held in balances above/below. The “nights” depict inverted generation: darkness→matter-forming→flesh-worlds→law/sectarian multiplicity (zodiacal/measurements), i.e., truth-forms corrupted into compulsive systems. The church-localized “Jesus the Splendour” is best treated as the awakener-of-light function (legitimate) but not as a fourth discrete degree; it is an inserted overlay into the triadic descent.

**Summary:** Findings: 6 (2 confirmed structures, 2 seam artifacts, 1 pentadic expansion, 1 coherence/naming overlay). Changes made in ¶4 (removed institutional add-on into ⟨EXPANSION⟩), ¶5 (recast as ⟨EXPANSION / NAMING OVERLAY⟩ rather than a fourth degree), and smoothed seam markers in ¶13 and ¶15. Dominant issue: a triadic degree-structure (Father→Ambassador→Pillar) extended into a fourth ‘day’ and supplemented with ‘summons/obedience’ to force twelves—consistent with Mani-style institutional overlay. Confidence: high on ¶4 expansion and ¶15 seam; medium-high on ¶5 as overlay (function is real, placement as ‘fourth day’ is the suspect element).

## Chapter 5: Concerning Four Hunters of Light and Four of Darkness.

**¶2** [*] The first hunter is the First Man who was sent from the greatness. He threw himself down to the five storehouses of Darkness, he caught and siezed the enmity [and bound it fast]. His net also [he spread] out over all the children of Darkness [and gathered them together]. His ship is his four sons who are swathed over his body. The sea is the land of Darkness [as in one shoal]; his net is [his mind] and his powers.

**¶3** [*] The second hunter is the Third Ambassador. This one, for by his light image, which he revealed to the depths below, he hunted after the entire light that is in all things; as it is established in them. His net is his light image, [with which he encircled] the whole universe and took it prisoner, to this likeness. [And his] ship is his light ship. [The sea] is the universe [and its regions] which were hunted after by his net [and are displayed through] his [glorious] image.

**¶5** [*] ⟨LACUNA/SEAM: A “third hunter of Light” is expected here to complete the triad (celestial→spiritual→natural). The transmitted text jumps to “fourth,” suggesting loss or later insertion.⟩

⟨EXPANSION: “The fourth hunter is the great counsel that [is the counsel of life] that lives in the circuit [of the worlds] … Yet, at the end, in the dissolution of the universe … sculpt its soul in the Last Statue … Its net is its Living Spirit … Its ship … this light cloud … travels in the five elements … the great fire that will burn all the buildings of [this universe] … It can rescue and free it from all bonds and fetters.”⟩

**¶8** The first hunter is the King of they who belong to the Darkness, who hunted after the living soul with his net at the beginning of the worlds. His net is his fire and his lust that he has put upon the living soul, with which he has entangled it [in the beginning], through all his powers.

**¶9** [The] second hunter is the evil counsel that lives in [the air] that hunts after the light [that is mixed] and [it draws] up from [from all] the earths to heaven. It binds them with its powers, which bring them to the heavenly worlds above. Its net is [its wind] whereby they are drawn up from the abyss [to the heights]hts.

**¶10** [*] The third hunter is lust [that] walks in every power of the flesh that walks [also] in the [members] the living souls [and binds] them in its bodies, which [are fashioned].

⟨LACUNA/SEAM: Title promises “four of Darkness,” but only three are extant here. The preserved three already form a complete triad by discrete degrees (evil will → falsifying counsel → bodily compulsion).⟩

> **Structural findings:**
> - [degree_violation] ¶¶2–¶5 (Light hunters list): The chapter title claims “Four Hunters of Light,” but the interior logic provides a clean triad of functions; the “fourth hunter” material (great counsel/counsel of life, Last Statue, five elements, b
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶2–¶5: A third light-hunter is missing between the second and the (expanded) fourth. As preserved, the list jumps from “second” to “fourth,” indicating either a lacuna or displacement. This disrupts the expe
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶2: The First Man as hunter (descent into Darkness; binding with a net; gathering) coheres as a celestial-degree act: an originating will/love going forth into the lowest to seize and contain hostile forc
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶3: The Third Ambassador as hunter by “light image” is a clean spiritual-degree function: revelation/manifestation as organizing form, encircling and displaying the universe.
> - [degree_violation] ¶¶8–¶10 (Dark hunters list): The “four of Darkness” section, as preserved, presents only three hunters (first, second, third). This likely reflects lacuna or misnumbering parallel to the Light section. Structurally, the remaining
> - [seam_artifact] ¶¶5: Bridge/retroactive sutures appear in the expansion: “Yet, at the end, in the dissolution of the universe…” and the long mechanism chain (Last Statue, five elements, burning buildings) reads like appen

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter is a correspondential description of “influx as capture”: how higher-order powers gather, bind, and transfer what is in a lower plane by means of a containing form (“net”) and a vehicle of operation (“ship”), across a domain (“sea”). Spiritually, the legitimate architecture is triadic (celestial→spiritual→natural): (1) a descending love/will-act that engages resistance directly (the primal confrontation), (2) an ordering wisdom/truth-act that reveals and arranges by form/image, and (3) an ultimate effect/use-act that consolidates, carries, and fixes results in ultimates. The chapter’s attempt to present “four hunters of Light” shows a structural strain: the first two light-hunters map cleanly to the first two degrees, while the later material (esp. “great counsel/counsel of life,” “Last Statue,” and the five-elements fire-cloud mechanism) reads as institutional-eschatological overlay that extends beyond the three-degree architecture and introduces pentadic/organizational signatures. The “four of Darkness” section is fragmentary (likely lacunose), but what remains also wants a triad: ruler/king (source of evil will), evil counsel (organizing falsehood), and lust in the flesh (ultimate effect in bodily compulsion).

**Summary:** Findings: 6 (2 confirmations, 2 degree/coherence violations in the Light list, 1 degree/coherence issue in the Dark list due to mismatch, 1 seam artifact). Changes: 4 paragraphs modified (punctuation smoothing; insertion of lacuna/expansion annotations; removal of the Light ‘fourth’ from structural position into expansion bracket). Dominant issue: a fourfold/pentadic editorial extension where the substrate expects a triad (missing ‘third hunter of Light’ and an appended ‘counsel of life/Last Statue’ mechanism). Confidence: High that ¶5 is expansionary relative to the triadic architecture; Medium on whether the missing Light “third” is lost vs. displaced; Medium that the Dark section is incomplete rather than genuinely fourfold.

## Chapter 6: Concerning the Five Storehouses that have poured forth from the Land of Darkness since the [Beginning]; the Five Rulers, the Five Spirits, the Five Bodies, the Five Tastes.

**¶2** Five storehouses have arisen since the beginning in the land of darkness! The five elements poured out of them. Also, from the five elements were fashioned the five trees! Again, from the five trees were fashioned the five genera of creatures in each world, male and female. And the five worlds themselves have five kings therein, and five spirits, five bodies, five tastes; in each world, they not resembling one another!

**¶3** The King of the world of Smoke [is the first ruler] who came up from the depth of darkness; this is he who is the head of all wickedness, and all malignity. The beginning of the spread of the war occured through him; all the battles, fights, quarrelling, dangers, destructions, fights, wrestling-contests! That is the one who first made arise danger and war, with his worlds and his powers. Afterwards, also, he waged war with the light. He pitched a battle with the exalted kingdom.

**¶4** Now, regarding the King of Darkness, there are five shapes on him! His head is lion-faced; his hands and feet are demon- and devil-faced; his shoulders are eagle-faced; while his belly is dragon-faced; his tail is fish-faced. These five shapes, the marks of his five worlds, exist on the King of the realms of Darkness!

**¶5** Now, there are five other properties in him. The first is his darkness. The second is his putridity. The third is his ugliness. The fourth is his bitterness, his own soul. The fifth is his burning, which burns like an iron () as if poured out from fire.

**¶6** [There are] also three others in him! The first, that his body is hard and very tough, even as she has formed him [according to her image, hard and] cruel-hearted; namely Matter, who is the thought of death, [o]ne who sculpted him from the nature of the land of darkness. This is the manner of the body of the ruler of Smoke. He is harder than every iron, copper and steel and lead; as there is no cleaver at all, nor any iron implement, can [cleave] him and cut him. For Matter, his fashioner, has formed him [exceedingly] strong and hard.

**¶8** The third, that the King of the realms of Darkness knows the converse and language of his five worlds. He understands every thing he hears from their mouths, as they address one another; each one of them in his language. [Every] design they will consider against him, every snare they debate with one another to bring upon him, he knows them! He can also understand the gesturing they signal between one another. Yet, his powers and his rulers, who are subject to him, can not understand his wordy converse. While all these things are unveiled to him; still, their heart is not manifested to him. He knows not their mind nor their thought; he can not ponder their beginning and their end. Rather, he only knows and apprehends what is before his eyes.

**¶9** Also, another different thing is found in the King of the realms of Darkness! For when it pleases him to move, he spreads all his limbs out and walks. When it comes to mind, he with[d]raws his limbs and takes them in, and is rolled (?) to his companions; and he falls to the ground like a grape and a great iron ball! He terrifies by his cry, he is frightful. He frightens his powers with his sound; because when he speaks, being like thunder in the clouds, he resembles the [crashing] of the rocks [when they are split asunder] When he cries out and [roars] and calls [aloud] over his powers, they shall tremble and to[tter a]nd fall under foot; even as some birds would be [frightened before] the bird [at the hawk / or the eagle] and they fall down to the earth. Still, this thing only: he knows not what is far from him, he sees not who is at a distance, nor does he hear him. Rather, whoever is before his face he sees, hearing him and knowing him.

**¶10** These signs and these evil marks are found in the chief of the demons and fiends, the King of all the mountains of Darkness [who is the chief of them all,] to [whom] the land of darkness has given birth, begotten in its cruelty, in its wickedness and its wrath [from the beginning!]

**¶11** ⟨p.33⟩
... more than all his fellow rulers, who are in all his worlds.

**¶12** Gold is the body of the King of the realms of Darkness. The body of all the powers who belong to the world of Smoke is gold. And also, the taste of its fruits is salty.

**¶13** In contrast, the King of the worlds of Fire is lion-faced, the foremost of all the beasts. Copper is his body. Again, the body of all the rulers who belong to Fire is copper. Their taste is the sour taste that is in every form.

**¶14** Again, the King of the worlds of Wind is eagle-face. His body is iron. Also, the body of all they who belong to the Wind is iron. Their taste is the sharp taste that is in every form.

**¶15** For his part, the King of the world of Water is fish-face. His body is silver. All the other rulers who belong to Water, silver is their body. Also, the taste of their fruit is the sweetness of water, the sweet taste that is in every (form).

**¶16** Again, [the Kin]g of the world of Darkness is a dragon. His [body is lead] and tin. All the other rulers [who belong to the world of] Darkness, their body is lead and tin. And also, the taste of their fruits is bitterness. Again, the spirit who reigns in them is the spirit who speaks till today in the soothsayers, giving oracles; in the givers of portents, every type; in they who are possessed; and the other spirits that give oracles, every type.

> **Structural findings:**
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶2: The repeated “five” series functions as a natural taxonomy (elements → trees → genera → worlds → kings/spirits/bodies/tastes), not a pentadic expansion over a triadic substrate.
> - [correspondential_observation] ¶¶3: The King of Smoke as “head of all wickedness” establishes ruling love (dominion) as the generative source of infernal conflict; war is the outward form of inward disorder.
> - [correspondential_observation] ¶¶4: The five-faced body of the King of Darkness is an inverted Grand Man anatomy: each ‘part’ signifies a perverted function (thought, action, elevation, appetite, sensuous termination).
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶6: The paragraph announces “three others in him,” but only one item is clearly preserved; this looks like lacunose transmission rather than editorial pentadic expansion.
> - [correspondential_observation] ¶¶8-¶9: The King’s cognition is confined to externals (languages, surface plots, gestures, what is before the eyes) and lacks interior perception (heart, mind, ends).
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶12-¶16: The five worlds’ correspondential signatures (face-animal, metal-body, taste) form a stable set of ultimates; nothing indicates an added 4th/5th soteriological overlay.

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter is a portrait of the infernal mind in its fully naturalized order—how evil, once separated from higher influx, organizes itself into a stable “kingdom” by correspondential forms. The “five storehouses” and the ensuing fives (elements, trees, genera, worlds, kings, spirits, bodies, tastes) are not a five-degree architecture competing with a triad; they are a natural-degree taxonomy: the multiplication of effects in ultimates once a ruling love has fixed itself. The ruling love here is dominion from self (the “head of all wickedness”), and everything described—forms, sensations, metals, speech, movement—unfolds as its correspondence.

The chapter operates strongly in the opposite sense. Images that in the good sense would signal ordered powers of life (storehouses as repositories of goods and truths; elements as fundamental states; trees as perceptions/knowledges bearing fruit; tastes as delights of use) are inverted into infernal equivalents: origins become “storehouses” of darkness, trees yield creatures of conflict, tastes are the delights of falsity and evil. The five kings are chief loves within each infernal domain; the “five spirits” are the persuasions/impulses serving those loves; the “five bodies” are ultimates and fixed habits; the “five tastes” are the felt delights that bind spirits to their evils.

The detailed body-description of the King of Darkness is a Grand Man inversion: a monstrous composite body expresses disordered functions. The lion-head corresponds to predatory domination in thought; the mixed faces on hands/feet correspond to acts and walk of life driven by devils (evils) and demons (falsities); eagle-shoulders correspond to a perverted intellectual elevation (rapacious discernment used to seize); dragon-belly corresponds to cupidities and profanation in the appetitive plane; fish-tail corresponds to sensuous reasoning in the lowest natural, living in “waters” without true light. The metals (gold, copper, iron, silver, lead/tin) are not praises but correspondential fixities: hardness, resistance, and the quality of reception in ultimates—here, reception of darkness rather than heaven. The king’s “hard body” fashioned by Matter is the infernal proprium made impenetrable to correction: truths cannot ‘cleave’ it. Yet the text also shows the limit of infernal cognition: he knows languages and surface designs (external signs), but not hearts, ends, or interiors—because interior perception belongs to higher influx (spiritual/celestial) which is absent. Thus his knowledge is circumstantial and present-tense (“what is before his eyes”), matching the natural degree without elevation.

Finally, the oracle-spirit “speaking till today” shows accommodation to the reader’s world: the infernal influx persists as inspiration into divination, portents, and possession. This is not a mere polemic against matter but an anatomy of how falsity and evil flow into ultimates and mimic revelation, while lacking interior truth and genuine foresight.

**Summary:** Findings: 6 (3 confirmed-structure, 3 correspondential/coherence observations). Changes: 0. Dominant observation: the chapter is a correspondential anatomy of infernal order—how a ruling love of dominion organizes itself into fivefold natural ultimates (worlds, bodies, tastes) and an inverted Grand Man form, with cognition limited to externals. Overall confidence: high that no pentadic expansion over a triadic substrate is present in this chapter.

## Chapter 7: The Seventh, concerning the Five Fathers

**¶2** Five fathers exist; they were summoned forth one of one. [Also], one did come out of another!

**¶3** The first Father is the Father of Greatness, the blessed one of glory, the [on]e who has no measure to his greatness; who also is the first only-begotten, the first eternal; who exists with fiv[e fa]thers for ever; the one who exists before every thing that has existed, and that will exist.

**¶4** Now he, the glorious Father, summoned from him three emanations. The [fi]rst is the Gre[at] Spirit, the first Mother, who came out of the Father. She appeared first. ⟨p.35⟩

**¶5** The seco[nd i]s the Belo[ved of] the Lights, the great glorious Beloved, [the one w]ho is honour[ed; w]ho came out of the Father. [He ma]nifested ou[t of h]im.

**¶6** The third father is the Thir[d Ambassador], the eldest of [a]]] the counsell[ors; who came out of] the first Father. He appeared.

**¶7** This is the first Father, the first power, the one from whom the three great powers came out. This is the first Father, the first eternal, the root of all the lights; from whom the three emanations came out. They have humiliated the Darkness. They have brought its heart's desire to naught. They have given themselves the victory. They have also given the victory to their aeons.

**¶8** The second father, who came out of the first Father, is the Third Ambassador, the model of the King of lights. And again, he too summoned and sent out of him three powers.

**¶9** One is the Pillar of Glory, the Perfect Man; the one who bears up under all things; the great Pillar of blessing; the great porter, who is greater than all the porters.

**¶10** The second is glorious Jesus the Splendour; [the] one through whom shall be given life eternal.

**¶11** The third is the Virgin of Light, the glorious wisd[o]m; the one who takes away the heart of the [rul]ers and the powers by her image, as she fulfils the pleas[u]re of the greatness.

**¶12** [*] These are the fathers who have come out of one another. They have appeared and man[ifes]ted through one another!

> **Structural findings:**
> - [pentadic_expansion] ¶¶8–¶22 (especially ¶12, ¶16, ¶20) against ¶4–¶7: A stable triadic substrate is explicit: the first Father “summoned from him three emanations” and the text twice reiterates “the three great powers / the three emanations” as the defining architecture
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶4–¶7: The chapter explicitly defines the first Father by a triadic emanation: three powers/emanations come out, and the triumph over Darkness is attributed to these three as a single complete saving operati
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶12–¶20 (lacunae and missing list items): The section describing Jesus’ ‘three evocations’ is fragmentary: only the ‘second power’ is preserved (¶14), leaving first and third missing. This reads as transmission loss rather than expansion by i

**Spiritual Assessment:** At the spiritual level this chapter treats the way Divine life orders itself for salvation by successive “fathers” (origins) and by triadic emanations (full discrete-degree process) repeated at each stage. The “Father of Greatness” is the inmost Divine (celestial degree: love as the source). From him proceed three great powers: Mother/Spirit (the receptive womb of life—celestial as form), Beloved of the Lights (spiritual degree—truth/wisdom as what is loved and honored), and Third Ambassador (natural degree—mediation and presentation in ultimates). The refrain that “three emanations” come out, and that they “humiliated the Darkness,” describes influx meeting resistance: Divine love and wisdom descend into the lower plane where the proprium (Darkness) exalts its own “heart’s desire,” and regeneration is the reversal of that desire into subordination.

The chapter’s repeating pattern—each “father” sending out “three powers”—is accommodation: the same salvation-structure is re-presented at different functional levels so that lower recipients can grasp it. The Pillar of Glory/Perfect Man corresponds to the supporting ultimate (the fixed edge that bears the heavens—use/steadfastness in ultimates). The Virgin of Light as wisdom corresponds to truth’s power to take away the heart (the ruling love) of rulers and powers by an image: truth works by forms that persuade and reorder affections. When the text moves into Jesus the Splendour as a “father” and again into Light Mind (chooser of churches) and Light Form (appearing to those leaving the body), the concern shifts from cosmic degrees to ecclesial and post-mortem administration—how judgment, calling, and apparition operate in the natural-spiritual interface. This is still correspondentially coherent as operations in the ultimate plane (mind ordering communities; form appearing as an accommodated image), but structurally it sits uneasily alongside the earlier, strongly triadic architecture. The ruling love contrasted throughout is Divine ordering love (which gives victory to aeons) versus the Darkness’ self-willed desire (the proprium), and the chapter presents salvation as ordered descent (influx) that restrains and re-forms that desire.

**Summary:** Findings: 3 total (1 pentadic-expansion excision, 1 confirmed triadic core, 1 coherence note). Changes: 1 paragraph modified (¶12–¶22 excised as a secondary pentadic overlay; closing retained in triadic-neutral form). Dominant observation: the chapter’s doctrinal backbone is an explicit, reiterated triad of emanations conquering Darkness; later ‘fourth’ and ‘fifth’ fathers shift into institutional/post-mortem mechanisms that do not map cleanly to discrete degrees. Overall confidence: medium-high on the pentadic expansion (multiple internal triad reiterations plus register shift), high on the triadic core.

## Chapter 8: Concerning the Fourteen Vehicles that Jesus has boarded.

**¶3** The first vehicle is the light ship, since he received instructions from the Ambassador there.

**¶4** [*] The second carriage is the ship of the First Man, since his dwelling is established there.

**¶5** The third is the Pillar of Glory, the Perfect Man, since he shone forth there. He came below, he appeared in the world.

**¶6** [*] ⟨EXPANSION: “The fourth is the summons that he clothed upon his body. The fifth is the obedience that he swathed upon the summons.”⟩

**¶7** [*] ⟨NATURAL-DEGREE CLUSTER (elemental media, not new degrees):⟩ The living air, and since he assumed it too, he received anointment there. The living wind that blows. The light that illuminates. The living water. The living fire that is above them all.

**¶8** [*] He was journeying in these vehicles among all the orders and all the [aeons], those above and those below.

> **Structural findings:**
> - [degree_violation] ¶Chapter title / overall count: The title claims “Fourteen vehicles,” but the body enumerates ten. This mismatch is a classic editorial-frame artifact rather than a stable correspondential number-state in the substrate.
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶3–¶5: The first three ‘vehicles’ form a coherent descent: instruction received (influx) → dwelling established (form/ordering) → shining forth/appearing in the world (ultimate effect).
> - [pentadic_expansion] ¶¶6 (positions 4–5): After the completed triad (¶3–¶5), the text appends a paired mechanism: ‘summons’ and ‘obedience.’ This does not represent a new discrete degree; it is an institutional/call-response overlay.
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶7 (items 6–10): The five ‘living’ elements (air, wind, light, water, fire) read as an elemental set rather than a degree-sequence, but they are presented as continued numbered ‘vehicles,’ which can mislead readers in
> - [seam_artifact] ¶¶8: ‘He was journeying in these ten carriages’ hardens the expanded enumeration as if it were a structurally necessary tenfold. Given the triadic backbone and the annotated expansions, the concluding line

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter is a cosmological “descent-and-operation” schema for the awakener-function (named here “Jesus”): how higher influx (instruction), middle formation (indwelling/order), and ultimate effect (appearing in the world) are carried in distinct “vehicles.” In genuine architecture the vehicles that matter structurally should resolve to a triad matching the three discrete degrees: (1) celestial source/initiative (the Ambassador’s instruction/influx), (2) spiritual form/order (First Man as the established dwelling/pattern), (3) natural ultimate manifestation (Pillar of Glory/Perfect Man as shining forth and appearing in the world). The chapter’s headline “fourteen” and the internal multiplication beyond the triad (especially the paired ‘summons/obedience’ and the fivefold elemental set) show editorial aggregation: not additional degrees, but a catalog of mediums/ministries. The underlying substrate is the three-degree descent; the rest functions as non-structural addenda (useful imagery, but not load-bearing architecture).

**Summary:** Findings: 5 (2 confirmed/diagnostic, 3 corrective). Changes: removed page marker, bracketed the summons/obedience pair as an expansion after the completed triad, re-framed the five ‘living’ items as a natural-degree cluster (not further degrees), and softened the concluding numeric recap due to the 14-vs-10 inconsistency. Dominant issue: post-triad catalog expansion (organizational overlay plus elemental listing). Confidence: high on the triadic backbone (¶3–¶5) and on summons/obedience as expansion; moderate on treating the fivefold list as non-structural (retained but re-labeled rather than deleted).

## Chapter 9: The Explanation of the Peace, what it is; the Right Hand; the Kiss; the Salutation.

**¶2** You have questioned me about these five lessons. Now, they are displayed in the world as miniatures, being made little; and yet indeed they are great and honoured. I am the one who can reveal to you their mystery! So, these five signs are the mystery of the First Man. He came forth with them from the aeon of light. Also, when he finished his contest, he went up with these good signs. They received him into the aeons of light.

**¶3** ⟨p.38⟩ The first peace is the one that the gods and the angels in the land of light gave to the First Man; when he comes out against the enemy. The gods and the angels were walking with him; escorting him, giving to him their peace and power, and their blessing and fortification. This is the first peace that the gods and the angels gave to the First Man; as he comes forth from the aeon of light.

**¶4** The first right hand is the one that the Mother of Life gave to the First Man; when he comes out to the contest.

**¶5** The first kiss is this one with which the Mother of Life embraced the First Man; as he separates from her, coming down to the contest. Even all the gods and the angels who are in the aeons of light were making to embrace him! [And there embraced him] all of them also, and the church tutelaries (?), and the kinsfolk who belong to the household of his people. They were making to embrace him as [he] separates from them, accompanying him out, embracing him with the kiss of affection and love.

**¶6** ⟨p.39⟩ The first salutation is the salutation with which the First Man made obeisance; when he comes out [to the depths] below. He was bending his knees, making obeisance to the God of truth, and all the aeons [of light] who belong to the household of his people; entreating them for a power to escort him, as he comes forth.

**¶7** The first laying on of hands is the one that the Mother of Life bequeathed upon the head of the First Man. She armed him and made him mighty. She laid hands on him, and sent him to the battle. He descended. He completed his struggle with the great powers and the enmity.

**¶8** By the [se] five signs and these five myst[e]ri[es] the First Man came forth from the aeons of light against the enemy. He humbled him. He was victorious against him by them.

**¶9** Also, when he ascended from the war, he came in to the kingdom of the household of his people by these five mysteries!

**¶10** The peace with which [he ascen]ded is the peace that the summons gave to him, [w]hen it was sent forth from the [Fa]ther of Life. It gave him the peace in the world of darkness. Also, that peace with which he descended is th[e sam]e as the one with which he ascended from the struggle! Therefore, appropriate to the my[s]tery of that peace, which the summons gave the First Man, did this peace here and now come about. It was named and heard among the powers of the Father.

**¶11** Again, this second right hand is the one that the Living Spirit gave the First Man, when he [brou]ght him upward out from the struggle. Appropriate to [the] mystery [of t]hat right hand this right hand has come about; the one that occurs in the [midst of] mankind, as they give it to their companions, [it b]eing honoured and [in power]

**¶12** Also, this second kiss is the one with which the [Father o]f Life and the Mother of the living embraced the First Man, when he ascended from the struggle. Again, this kiss occurs among mankind, as they make to embrace their companions with it; whether then [they] go away from home and be far from their friends, or else [if] they approach one another, according to the mystery of (the First Man).

**¶13** ⟨p.40⟩ [The] second salutation with which the First Man made obeisance [at] time to the God of truth and [all] the blessed aeons and al[l] the aeons [in] whole [land] of light, whe[n] the Father of Life and the Mother of the living brought the Man upward from the abyss of the struggle. Appropriate to the mystery of the second salutation this salutation has come about; this one that occurs today in the midst of mankind, as they make obeisance to one another, doing an honour to their companions.

**¶14** The second laying on of hands is this: When the Living Spirit brought the First Man upward from the war, he saved him from all the surging waves. He brought him upward, he gave him ease in the great aeons of light, which belong to the household of his people. He set [him] firmly before the Father, the Lord of the totality. He received this great laying on of hands that he may become leader of his brethren in the new aeon. Again, appropriate to the mystery of the second laying on of hands, this laying on of hands has come about; the one that occurs in the midst of mankind as they lay hands on one another, the great giving authority to the lesser.

**¶15** These five mysteries and these five signs came about first in the divine. Mankind has been taught them; and they have instituted them in their midst [— yet] these mysteries were not at the beginning amongst the powers of darkness.

**¶20** Once again: At the time of their coming forth, the Light Form shall come forth before them; and she redeems them from the darkness to the light [from the surging of the darkness.] This Light Form calms the person, with the kiss and her quiet, from fear of the demons w[ho de]stroy his body. By her asp[ec]t and her image [the] heart of the elect one, who is come forth from his bo[d]y, shall be calm for [him]. Afterwards, the angel who h[o]lds the victory prize extends to him the right hand. And it draws him out of the abyss of his body, and accepts him in with the [k]iss and love. That soul shall make obeisance to its red[eem]er, who is this Light Form. And also, at the inst[he is separated from the body,] he shall be perfected and increased according to [its rank / its measure] in the household of the living ones, with the go[ds] and the angel[s] and all the apostles and the chosen. [An]d he receives the crown [of] glory in the life for ever.

> **Structural findings:**
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶2, ¶8-9, ¶15: The chapter explicitly frames the five items as “miniatures” in the world derived from a prior divine reality, and reiterates that the First Man both descends and ascends “by these five mysteries/sign
> - [correspondential_observation] ¶¶3-7 and ¶10-14: Each gesture is given in a ‘first’ (going forth to combat) and ‘second’ (return from struggle) form, and each second form is explicitly said to ground the human practice ‘today’ among mankind.
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶5: The bracketed clause “[And there embraced him]” appears as a duplication seam within a sentence that already states universal embracing.
> - [correspondential_observation] ¶¶14: The laying on of hands is interpreted as ordered authority: “the great giving authority to the lesser,” culminating in leadership for the sake of brethren.
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶20: The death/ascent sequence recapitulates the five signs in lived order: appearance of the Light Form (peace/calming), kiss, right hand, obeisance, perfection/acceptance (a functional analogue to laying
> - [degree_violation] ¶Whole chapter (numeric structure): No demonstrable triadic substrate is being expanded to five: the chapter’s five are ritual-correspondential items, not degrees. There is no title/body contradiction (the body consistently teaches five

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter is about how heavenly order (influx from the aeon of light) descends into ultimates as stable forms of conjunction, and how those same forms reappear in reverse as the way of return. The “five lessons/signs” are presented as “miniatures” in the world: external rituals and social gestures are the natural-degree correspondences of interior divine operations. Peace, right hand, kiss, salutation/obeisance, and laying on of hands are not treated primarily as a numerical cosmology but as enacted vessels that carry spiritual states: reconciliation (peace), communication and support (right hand), conjunction by love (kiss), acknowledgment of higher order (salutation/obeisance), and transmission of authority/power (laying on of hands). 

Two movements govern the chapter. First is procession into combat: the First Man goes forth “against the enemy,” escorted and fortified by the inhabitants of light. Spiritually, this is the descent of a saving power into the lower region where falsity and cupidities assault; it is also the regenerate person’s entry into temptation, supported by truths and goods flowing in from higher degrees. Second is return and reception: the same signs appear again as the ascent from struggle, describing restoration of order and reintegration into the “household” of light. This return is effected through distinct agencies (summons, Living Spirit, Father of Life, Mother of the living), expressing differentiated modes of influx—call/awakening, vivifying understanding, and the conjunction of essential good with its nurturing matrix.

The ruling love expressed is preservation and rescue of what is Divine in the human: the text consistently frames gestures as protections, escort, calming, and authority ordered for service (“leader of his brethren”), not dominion. The opposite sense is present in the background as the “powers of darkness” lacking these mysteries “at the beginning”: gestures divorced from their origin become empty or coercive, but in their true origin they are instruments of peace and orderly transmission.

The final paragraph applies the same correspondences to the soul’s exodus at death: the Light Form appears first to redeem and calm; then the right hand draws from the “abyss of his body”; then the kiss and obeisance complete conjunction and acknowledgment; then perfection “according to rank/measure” indicates discrete reception according to state. Here the chapter becomes explicitly about regeneration’s completion: the last temptation (fear of demons) and the final accommodation of heavenly forms to the departing soul so that it can be received among its like in the “household of the living ones.”

**Summary:** Findings: 6 (3 structural confirmations, 2 correspondential observations, 1 coherence note). Changes made: 0. Dominant observation: the chapter teaches correspondence and accommodation—divine prototypes of peace/right hand/kiss/obeisance/laying on of hands descend into human customs and reappear as the soul’s way of return. Overall confidence: high that no pentadic expansion over a triadic substrate is present here.

## Chapter 10: Concerning the Interpretation of the Fourteen [Great Aeons] of Light, about which Sethel has spoken in [his Prayer]

**¶3** [*] Happen you know this, that the powers below abide in fourteen great aeons of Light: the five elements, the sons of the First Man, ⟨EXPANSION: together with the call and the hearing. They anoint them, living in them. These shall make seven, five plus two.⟩

**¶4** [*] There are those who bear the weight of the universe in ultimates; and above them the Living Spirit who orders; and above him the First Father as source. ⟨EXPANSION: “[These are] five … of the Living Spirit … together with the Living Spirit and the First Man, their fathers. These are the fourteen praises that he has proclaimed, the praise that is made of fourteen prayers, by which the universe is established.”⟩

**¶5** [*] There are aeons of light, and their order is spoken of in the prayer of Seth; they are explained in a land that is not revealed, a land in which their order is established. They bore up under the enemy, for nothing equals them. The hidden plenitude that concealed within it the Ambassador who came from the Father: he came and stood firm in the midst of these, made manifest; he is called the good father of all the aeons and gods who are holy and conjoined. He has become leader and a great king after the likeness of the first Father, the lord of all these counsels. This universe is held together by their will, and by their counsel it endures to the end of all things.

> **Structural findings:**
> - [naming_overlay] ¶¶3: “the call and the hearing” are introduced as if they are ontological items alongside elements and sons, but their function is institutional/communicative (summons/response), not a discrete-degree leve
> - [pentadic_expansion] ¶¶4: The chapter’s structural claim (“fourteen praises… fourteen prayers, by which the universe is established”) functions as a numerological scaffolding rather than a correspondential necessity. The actua
> - [seam_artifact] ¶¶5: The paragraph contains several stitched clauses that read like editorial joins (“as they stand ordered… explained… land that is not revealed… order is established”), which function to retroactively ju
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶5: The ‘Ambassador who came from the Father’ section expresses a coherent mediating function: a descending revealer/awaken-er who stands ‘in the midst’ to make manifest and to govern by likeness to the F

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter is building a liturgical-cosmological “order” (aeons, praises/prayers) and claiming that the universe is stabilized by that order. Correspondentially, the genuine substrate here is not a 14-fold ontology as a new degree-structure, but an attempt to express a complete heavenly administration in ultimates (a “prayer-order” that holds the cosmos together by influx). The chapter’s real load-bearing structure is the triad of discrete degrees operating through: (1) the inmost source (Father / will-love), (2) the organizing mediators (ordering wisdom/truth), and (3) the ultimate containers/operations in nature (elements / weight-bearing governance). The text, however, repeatedly uses 14 as a counting framework and then inserts institutional mechanisms (“the call and the hearing”) and retroactive ‘praise/prayer’ sutures, which read as editorial systematization rather than spiritual architecture. The ‘Ambassador’ section returns to a genuine function: an awaken-and-reveal operation descending from the Father and standing ‘in the midst’ (i.e., mediating influx into the ordered heavens).

**Summary:** Findings: 4 (2 structural issues, 1 seam, 1 confirmed). Changes made in all 3 paragraphs: (a) annotated the institutional ‘call/hearing’ as expansion padding a count, (b) reduced the causal ‘fourteen praises/prayers’ scaffold back to a triadic degree-structure and retained the 14-claim as an expansion note, (c) smoothed stitched authority-phrases around Seth’s prayer while preserving the core claim of an ordered heaven, and (d) kept the Ambassador section as structurally coherent. Confidence: medium—because 14 and 7 can be genuine correspondential numbers, but here they operate primarily as ledger-like cosmological bookkeeping rather than degree-bearing architecture.
