## Chapter 37: Concerning the Three Zones.

**¶2** [*] When he built and constructed the worlds, he suspended the zones upon their zones, and one within another. ⟨NOTE: The chapter title signals a triadic architecture (‘three zones’), but the surviving text names only ‘the first zone’ explicitly; the remaining zones are implied by the suspension/nesting logic in ¶3–¶5.⟩ The first zone is the five powers of darkness, males and females.

**¶3** This is why he hung one zone on a zone! He hung the five powers of Darkness on the five shining men, the sons of the Man; because all the enmity is entwined and bound in them.

**¶4** [*] Within the zone are the five sons of the ⟨Living⟩ Spirit; because the entire edifice is suspended from them. ⟨SEAM: the damaged clause about how “others were hung after them” appears to be a retroactive ordering suture and is not required by the discrete-degree logic.⟩

**¶5** [*] And the one set over the firmaments above the place that is in one zone is the great ⟨King of Honour⟩, who exists and is set within the ⟨treasury and boundary⟩ of the universe. ⟨NOTE: ‘treasury/boundary’ is the functional marker here (ultimates that contain and preserve); the name ‘King of Honour’ rides on that function. The opening verb phrase is too fragmentary to further normalize without guessing.⟩

> **Structural findings:**
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶2: The title announces “Three Zones,” but ¶2 immediately defines “the first zone” as a pentad (“five powers of darkness”) without ever clearly naming zones two and three. The chapter’s internal architect
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶3: The ‘hanging’ of the five powers of Darkness upon the five shining men is functionally coherent: hostility is bound by a restraining/combative mediator.
> - [seam_artifact] ¶¶4: The paragraph opens with a bridge connective (“Also,”) and then contains damaged retroactive-suture language (“it being [also as was]ard (?) him too, the oth[others were hung] after them”) that reads 
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶5: ¶5 introduces the ‘great King of Honour’ located in the ‘treasury and boundary of the universe,’ but the sentence is heavily fragmented and unclear in agency (“And this is the one who came to [behold,
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶2–¶4: The repeated ‘suspension’ and ‘within’ language coherently encodes containment-by-degrees (zones nested and supported).

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter describes cosmic “suspension” as correspondential architecture: higher-order goods and truths contain, bind, and hold lower-order disorder so a stable arena for regeneration can exist. Spiritually, “zones on zones” is discrete-degree containment (interior within exterior) and the Grand-Man principle that ultimates are held in form by prior causes. The substrate signal here is not a triad of persons but a triad of FUNCTIONS: (1) what must be contained (disorder/hostile impulses: ‘powers of darkness’), (2) what contains by combat and restraint (the mediating martial function: ‘shining men, sons of the Man’), and (3) what contains by construction and ongoing support (the architectural/ordering function: ‘sons of the Living Spirit’). The repeated “five” sets read as quantitative groupings (sufficient/much), not as an added fourth/fifth degree; the real structural claim is the vertical nesting and suspension (influx and containment), not a pentadic emanation.

**Summary:** Findings: 5 (2 confirmed structures, 3 issues). Changes: 3 paragraphs lightly modified (¶2, ¶4, ¶5) by annotating the missing explicit triad, removing a bridge connective and isolating a likely seam-suture, and foregrounding function over name in a fragmented line. Dominant issue: the chapter title’s ‘three’ is architecturally real but not explicitly enumerated in the surviving text, while ‘five’ appears as quantitative groupings (not additional degrees). Confidence: medium (text is fragmentary in ¶4–¶5; harmonization avoided reconstructive guessing).

## Chapter 38: Concerning the Light Mind and the Apostles and the Saints

**¶1** [*] You have told us that the Light Mind is this one who shall come and assume the saints. You said it to us like this: He too is one among the gods and many gods are with him. When he enters within the body of the flesh and binds the old man, he sets governance upon the body in its outer functions. ⟨EXPANSION: “with his five counsels… he sets his five counsels upon him in the five limbs of his body.”⟩

**¶2** So, now, where is he? In that the old man is chained in the body! For I see how rebellions arise there despite his bondage, from time to time.

**¶3** Also, secondly, I ask you: If then he is a great God, unchanging and immeasurable, how could he come and appear in the smallness of the body?

**¶4** The third thing I want you to recount to me: How a holy one is the Mind, and he is pure; how does he not receive the defilement of this body?

**¶5** Fourthly: If truly the Light Mind exists in the saints, why is his likeness not displayed to us, the way that he is?

**¶6** The fifth thing I want, for you to tell me and explain about your apostolate. Look, it is not clear to me! For they oppress and persecute you in the world.

**¶9** All that you have unveiled you have spoken in our presence. Yet, I wanted to know, and know the Light Mind, the way that he is.

**¶10** If I shall repeat again to you about these things that you are so set upon, and they become true for you after you are so set upon them, will you understand what you seek to understand? On your account I will give vision to they who see! I will make the living fountain overflow for the thirsty, that they may drink and live.

**¶11** [*] All the error, when the enemy of the lights constructed it, he constructed after the likeness of a man. The head of the universe is the beginning of the garments. His neck is the nape of the garments. His stomach is the five unfolded ones (?), which are the folds of the garments. His ribs are all the firmaments. His navel is the sphere of the stars and the signs of the zodiac. The parts that come from his navel to his hip are the extensions that come from the sphere to the corners of the four worlds. His loins are the three earths that are below that are set upon the head of the Porter. His thighs are the breadth to the earth upon which the Porter is stood firm. His shins and his feet are the mountains and the entire zone that belongs to the four quarters.

**¶12** [*] His heart is mankind. His liver is the four-footed animals. His lung is the race of birds that fly in the air. His spleen is the race of fish that swim in the waters. His kidneys are the world of reptiles that creep upon the earth. His outer skin is the wall that surrounds the piercing and the great fire. His vessels are the vessels of the great fire. His inner skin is of darkness. ⟨UNCERTAIN / SEAM: broken organ-series continues: gall… intestines… midriff… veins/bladder… springs and wells… ears/winds… feet/toes.⟩

**¶13** [*] This is how each of the worlds have been harmonised (?). Governing powers are fastened in him. ⟨EXPANSION: “Five gods are fastened in him.”⟩ [and made to stand] they are his soul and his life [and bound together] man is a sinner [for] the Living Spirit and the Ambassador [until the bonds are loosed] the five sleepless guardians.

**¶14** [*] He has set in him the Keeper of Splendour (the interior mind set fast above). He has enclosed the entire of the powers of heaven that are in the camp of the great King of Honour (the ruling thought in the firmament). He has set over them the Adamant of Light (insight). He has appointed the King of Glory (counsel and patience) over the three images: that of wind, that of fire, and that of water. He has appointed the Porter (the ultimate gate-consideration) as foundation below all the earths.

**¶15** [*] Furthermore: the Ambassador refined from them intellectual forms of life for governance. ⟨EXPANSION: “five intellectuals of life. Also, the summons and the obedience were situated there.”⟩ Now, they made six sons of the Living Spirit, together with the six sons of the First Man!

**¶16** Further: The Ambassador placed in them the great Mind; who is the Pillar of Glory, the Perfect Man.

**¶17** Furthermore: Jesus the Youth was set there; who is the image of the living word, of the utterance and the obedience.

**¶18** He has made strong these camps, those above and those below (so that) each one of them will be secure in the circuit of his watch-district, so that neither uprising nor treachery are made in his watch-district.

**¶19** And look, see! The Keeper of Splendour is set firm in the great mind, in the camp above the prison of the bound ones, for he brings to nothing all the gloom of death. And a treachery came about, and an uprising! The Sin aborted, it tangled in with the soul. It became mixed with this light that it expelled toward the image of the Ambassador. It rose in the third firmament that is above the watch-tower of the Keeper of Splendour. From that place also it tangled in with the light. It was detached and came down to that which is dry and that which is moist. It fashioned the trees upon the dry (land); but in the sea it immediately took form and made a great uprising in the sea.

**¶20** Again, look, see! The great King of Honour, who is the thought, he is in the third firmament. He is made with wrath. And an uprising came about! A treachery and an anger happened in his camp. The watchers of heaven, who came down to the earth in his watch-district, they did all the deeds of treachery. They have revealed crafts in the world and have unveiled to people the mysteries of heaven. An uprising came about, and a destruction, on the earth, and ruin was added to it.

**¶21** The Adamant of Light is the fulfillment. He is set firm upon the fifth firmament and a treachery came about in his camp; the occasion when the abortions fell to the earth. They formed Adam and Eve. They begat them so as to reign through them in the world. They fashioned every object of lust upon the earth. The entire world was filled by their lust.

**¶22** Once again: In the watch of the great King of Glory, who is the great counsel, he who exercises authority over the three wheels. A disturbance came about, and an affliction, for they were greatly pained and oppressed in the three earths. After the Ambassador displayed his image, the paths closed to their ways; their ascent was impeded by them, and the wind, the water, and the fire ascend on them.

**¶23** Once again: In the watch of the Porter, he who humiliates the great uprising of the abysses below, the fastenings underneath were loosened in the foundation below.

**¶24** On account of the earthquake that happened in the watch of the Keeper of Splendour, the Pillar of Glory came forth, as helper of the Keeper of Splendour. It bore up under all burdens.

**¶25** Conversely, because of the treachery and the uprising that happened in the watch of the great King of Honour, which is the watchers who came down to earth from the heavens; four angels were called upon about them. They bound the watchers with an eternal chain, in the prison of the blackened ones (?). They obliterated their children from upon the earth.

**¶26** Then, again, the abortions descended in the watch of the Adamant and begat Adam and Eve; because of that great treachery which happened, and the mystery of wickedness, he sent Jesus the Youth, the prayer of the five sons. He assumed them through the abortions. He fastened them beneath the mind of Adam.

**¶27** Also, because of the earthquake that happened in these three earths, and in that the paths were hindered and the springs of wind and water and fire were impeded, Jesus cast himself down. He assumed Eve; and he straightened the tracks of the wind, the water, and the fire. He opened the springs for them, and he set in order the path of their ascent.

**¶28** Once again: Since the earth beneath the Porter was loosened from the fastenings that were beneath it because of this too Jesus went down, assuming Eve until he reached that place. He set in order and strengthened the fastenings beneath. He returned, he came up to his place of rest.

**¶29** Look at all these watches of the zone, the ones in which these great gods are master, watching over them in their stations. Uprisings have happened, and treachery, in them. The earthquakes occurred in them from time to time; with a great humiliation, until they humbled the powers of the enmity.

**¶30** So also is this body! A mighty power lives here, even if it is small in its stature. Nevertheless, Sin dwells within, and the old man who is lodged in it. Certainly he is cruel, with great cunning; until the Light Mind finds how to humble this body, and drive it according to his pleasure.

**¶31** Just so, in the watch-districts of his great outer brothers, they who are masters in the zone: in them, in the great body, the earthquake and treachery happened from time to time. So, also, is the watch-district of the Mind, which is the body of the flesh. Sin raises up, from time to time, its agitations in the body.

**¶32** Now you, understand thus, that the powers of light are good. The beginning and the end are unveiled to them. All that they do is being done with correct judgement. Indeed, because of this they may permit the enmity to initiate error, and do its pleasure for a moment. Then they seize them afterward; they have acted first with a righteous judgement.

**¶33** And with respect to this other (question) that you have put forward: How may the Light Mind come, this great and honoured mighty one, and assume this little body of the flesh? Again, look, see! These gods, in that are great and mighty ones. And each one of them is enclosed and hard pressed in this place wherein he is set; like trees holding to their taproot. So also this is how each one of them has held to his taproot in the world, according to the kind of place in which he is set, bearing up till [that are in the zone].

**¶35** [*] ⟨NOTE: the following fivefold mappings describe a natural-degree sufficiency set (operative faculties in ultimates), not additional discrete degrees.⟩
(Sin) bound (the soul) in the five members of the body. It bound the mind in bone; the thought in sinew; the insight in vein; the counsel in flesh; the consideration in skin.

**¶36** (Sin) set fast its five powers: its mind upon the mind of the soul; its thought upon the thought of the soul; its insight upon the insight of the soul; its counsel upon the counsel of the soul; its consideration upon the consideration of the soul. It placed its five angels and authorities upon the five members of the soul, which it had brought in and bound in the flesh. They were standing there, pronouncing to the soul and continually leading it on to every evil thing, to all the sins of lust, to the worship of idols, to erroneous opinions, to humiliation; in the humiliation of slavery! As it is set fast, worshipping the things that are made of matter will not remain; making obeisance to idols of wood and gold and silver and stones; worshipping beasts that are unclean and polluted! They are ugly in their appearances and their forms.

**¶37** (The soul) assumed error and forgetfulness. It forgot its essence and its race and its kindred; knowing not the door of the place to pray to him, nor call up to him. It became hostile to its father and it hated itself, being wicked and hostile to its own light.

**¶38** The Light Mind comes and finds the soul lying in forgetfulness; and he raises its wisdom in the body. He shall become for it a loosener of bonds of members in the body. He shall loosen the mind of the soul and release it from the bone. He shall release the thought of the soul from the sinew; and so bind the thought of the sin in the sinew. He shall release the insight of the soul from the vein; and so bind the insight of the sin in the vein. He shall loosen the counsel of the soul, and release it from the flesh; and so bind the counsel of the sin in the flesh. He shall release the consideration of the soul from the skin; and bind the consideration of the sin in the skin.

**¶39** This is how he shall release the members of the soul, and make them free from the five members of sin. Conversely, these five members of sin, which were loose, he shall bind them.

**¶40** He shall set right the members of the soul; form and purify them, and construct a new man of them, a child of righteousness. And when he fashions and constructs and purifies the new man; then he shall bring forth five great living members out from the five great members. And he places them in the members of the new man. He shall place his mind, which is love, in the mind of the new man. Also, the thought, which is faith, he shall place in the thought of the new man, whom he shall purify. His insight, which is perfection, he shall place in the insight of the new man. His counsel, which is patience, he shall place in his counsel. Also, wisdom, which is his consideration, in the consideration of the new man. He shall make the image of the word pure from the word of sin; and he adds to it his word, so that his utterance becomes nourisher and strengthener.

**¶41** Now, when he shall perfect and order all the twelve members. This happens by his love and his wisdom. His faith becomes righteous, as he perfects him in completeness. While formerly he was running in wandering ways, but instead of this, now he runs in the straight way, his road and his path and his work; also he mounts up to the heights, to the great aeons. Thus the old man is bound in the body, and his lust, his foolishness in these five members of the body; the dark spirit is imprisoned with them in a bond and in severe misery.

**¶42** Also, the new man reigns by his love, by his faith, by his perfection, by his patience, and by his wisdom. Yet, his king is the Light Mind, he who is king over the totality. He reigns over it according to his pleasure. While the members are secure, thus sin is imprisoned. Still, the Light Mind is king, and an affliction may arise in the body from time to time.

**¶43** There are occasions when sin shall mount up with its foolishness; and it disturbs the consideration, and it clouds the wisdom and the understanding of the person. It causes truth to be split into doubt in him, and he utters some foolish words and some empty ones.

**¶51** Then, have I taught you, and opened your eyes, as to how confusion shall arise in the zone, in the camps of these great mighty gods? Again, this is how disturbance shall come about, from time to time, in the watch-districts of the Light Mind.

**¶52** So also these watch-keepers, for they are set fastened firm in the zone, but they are not visible. Again, this too is the way of the Light Mind, for he is not visible in the body.

**¶53** Also, corresponding to these outer watch-keepers: in that while they are great, yet have they contorted themselves. They have bent themselves down, they have become small, to the measure of the task that is apportioned to each one of them. Again, this too is like the Mind, himself a great one, and exalted; but he is bent over and has become small, to this little worthless body.

**¶54** Even so the gods in the outer zone: they are transcendent and purified, they are set fast in the mingling of the totality, but are not defiled. This too is like the Light Mind, while he is placed in his place in the flesh.

**¶55** Again, behold the mightiness and the activity of the Light Mind: how vast he is over all the watch-districts of the body! He stays fast at his camp. He shuts off all deliberations of the body from the beguilements of Sin. He limits them and distributes them out. He sets them down at his pleasure.

**¶57** I have taught you the deeds of the Light Mind! Whoever has an open and perceptive eye, he can appear to him. Whoever has not that eye, he can not appear to him.

> **Structural findings:**
> - [pentadic_expansion] ¶¶1: Five counsels placed into five limbs is presented as if it were the governing cosmological mechanism of the Light Mind’s indwelling. The chapter later runs on triadic structural logic (three earths; t
> - [seam_artifact] ¶¶11: Bridge connective ‘And, also,’ marks a continuation inside a long body-cosmos mapping; it likely stitches heterogeneous fragments (macro-body mapping + four-world corner extensions) rather than flowin
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶12: The organ-to-kingdom mapping (heart=mankind; liver=four-footed; lung=birds; spleen=fish; kidneys=reptiles) is functionally plausible as a natural-degree schema, but the paragraph becomes textually uns
> - [pentadic_expansion] ¶¶13: ‘Five gods are fastened in him’ introduces a pentadic cosmic fastening inside a passage that otherwise argues by triadic ‘zones/earths’ and watch-district governance. This is a classic pentarchy overl
> - [naming_overlay] ¶¶14–18: The named ‘watch-keepers’ largely function as stationed governance principles across degrees (mind above; thought; insight; counsel/patience over wind/fire/water; porter/threshold below). Names are ac
> - [pentadic_expansion] ¶¶15: ‘Five intellectuals of life’ plus ‘summons and the obedience’ is an additive institutional seam. The ‘summons-and-obedience’ mechanism is a known Mani signature occupying extra slots beyond the triad 
> - [pentadic_expansion] ¶¶35–36, ¶38–40, ¶42: The repeated fivefold faculty/tissue mapping (mind/thought/insight/counsel/consideration bound into bone/sinew/vein/flesh/skin; then reversed by the Light Mind; then ‘five living members’ love/faith/p
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶30–32, ¶51–55, ¶57: The macrocosm-to-microcosm application, the doctrine of permitted disturbances under righteous judgment, accommodation (‘becoming small’), invisibility of interior governors, and perception by an open

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter is structurally about lawful influx and regeneration: how a higher ruling principle (the Light Mind = spiritual-degree wisdom/understanding proceeding into ultimates) can dwell in a “small” natural body without defilement, and why disturbances still arise. The chapter’s genuine architecture is triadic in its core logic (source → form → effect; interior watch → mediating governance → ultimate restraint), and it repeatedly uses the Grand Man correspondence (macrocosm mirroring microcosm) to explain temptation as periodic “uprisings” at the boundaries.

However, Mani’s editorial hand is strongly present in (a) pentadic institutional/anthropological schemata (five counsels, five limbs, five gods, five intellectuals, summons-and-obedience; and the fivefold soul-members mapped to tissues), and (b) retrofitting of named agents into extra slots that do not correspond to additional discrete degrees. The chapter itself “bleeds” structural threes (three earths; three images/wheels; repeated triadic descent-ascent logic), while the fivefold taxonomies function organizationally rather than architecturally. Where the fivefold sets can be read as a natural-degree ‘sufficiency’ set (faculties in ultimates), they can stand; where they are asserted as cosmic/load-bearing governance (“five gods fastened in him,” “five intellectuals of life” plus “summons and obedience”), they overrun the three-degree architecture and should be marked as expansion.

The most genuine substrate is the macrocosm–microcosm argument: as watch-districts in the “outer body” experience permitted disturbances, so the human body/mind does; the Light Mind governs by restraint and redistribution, is not visible as an object, and is perceived only by an opened understanding. This aligns tightly with discrete degrees and influx: the higher remains pure, acts within the lower by correspondence, and does not become ‘small’ in itself—only accommodated in reception.

**Summary:** Findings: 8 total (3 pentadic expansions flagged, 2 seam/coherence issues, 1 naming-overlay clarification, 1 confirmed-structure confirmation, 1 fivefold-set reclassification note). Changes made: localized smoothing of a seam connective (¶11), three expansion annotations (¶1, ¶13, ¶15), one uncertainty annotation for fractured organ-series (¶12), and one functional gloss for watch-keepers (¶14) plus a guard-note preventing misreading fivefold sets as degrees (¶35). Dominant issue: pentadic taxonomies and summons/obedience insertions that exceed the three-degree architecture while the chapter’s main argument remains triadic-influx coherent. Confidence: high on identifying Mani-style expansions in ¶1/¶13/¶15; medium on ¶12 as seam/fragment due to lacunae; high on confirming the chapter’s core spiritual architecture (macrocosm–microcosm, accommodation, invisibility by degree).

## Chapter 39: Concerning the Three Days and the Two Deaths

**¶4** The first day is our father, the Man. From the time when the First Man went down to the abyss of darkness and drowned in it; and the Living Spirit was sent, who went and bore him up. After the Man had ascended, the Father of Life and the Great Spirit set things in order, and the worlds above and below.

**¶5** Furthermore, the glorious Ambassador came and dwelt in the ship of the day. He displayed his glorious image, and he purified the light out of all things. He walked in the heights of the heavens. Till the time when the world will be dissolved, all things destroyed, the great fire released, and the Last Statue ascends. These three deeds were begun on this first day, when they sent the First Man with his five sons.

**¶6** ⟨p.103⟩ In contrast, [the first death] is the darkness that mixed with the five sons of the First Man.

**¶7** The second day is the time when the fathers of light, who were victorious in the struggle, will sit on their thrones in the new aeon and dwell on the new earth. And they reign in the new aeon till the Father unveils his image above them.

**¶8** The {length of the sojourn} in the new aeon equals the measure of the first day spent in affliction. They will reign like this and rejoice in the new aeon.

**¶9** [*] The great third day [is the time] when the Father will unveil over them his image ... ... and he raises them up [and he shows them the glory of his greatness] and he receives them into his hidden treasury ... and he gives [from their aeons into his light] his soul. He pours out upon them ... his ambrosia and his sweet perfume, which takes away all affliction that they have seen with their eyes. [to them from] fullness and a joy eternal! When his grace sates them, he gives to them his [gift], and they are made anew in him.

**¶10** [*] He can summon them once more in peace and silence, and perfect their stature in same measure as his first aeons. And he sets each power in its aeon, in its dwelling-place. They will dwell in the new aeon in their aeons!

**¶11** Now, when they dwell in their aeons, they will become rich beyond measure, for ever, beyond time. From then on they will not count that season amongst them; nor the number of the days, nor the hours, generation after generation, for ever and ever. The Father will not be hidden from them, from this time on. Rather, he has accomplished (his) unveiling to them [in the great third day] he being revealed to them for ever.

**¶12** These are the three great days, [which the Father of Greatness has given number to in the reckoning].

**¶13** In contrast, the two deaths that I have proclaimed to you are these.

**¶14** ⟨p.104⟩ The first death is from the time when the light fell to the darkness, and was mixed in with the rulers of darkness; until the time when the light will become pure, and be separated from the darkness in that great fire. The remainder left behind there can build and add to that Last Statue.

**¶15** [*] Now, the second death is the death in which the souls of sinful men shall die; when [they will] be stripped of the shining light that illuminates the world. They are separated from the living air, from which they receive [living] breath; and they are deprived of this living soul, which [was set] in the world; and they are separated and swept away from this Last Statue [to the outer darkness] and bound on [their bonds] the female will become [a fire] their torture and their affliction; for their deeds [will lead them into] those three places;

**¶16** These are the two deaths. The first death is temporal; [but] the second death is eternal. It is the sec[ond deat]h!

> **Structural findings:**
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶4–¶12: The chapter’s “three days” form a complete triad and behave like a genuine discrete-degree sequence rather than an expanded taxonomy.
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶13–¶16: The “two deaths” are a coherent duality: cosmic mixture/separation vs. personal deprivation/binding, not a pentadic overlay.
> - [seam_artifact] ¶¶15: Bridge connective “And also” functions as a stitch within a piling-up clause chain, not as a new structural item; it can be removed to restore clean flow.
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶9: One phrase is textually jagged (“and he gives [from their aeons into his light] his soul. He can pour out upon them ...”) and contains a duplicated conjunction later (“his [and] and”). This reads like

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter is a clean example of genuine triadic architecture: it lays out “three days” as a complete descent-and-return cycle across the discrete degrees, and “two deaths” as two distinct states of separation.

Structurally, the three days map to the three degrees by function:
1) First day: engagement with darkness and the ordering of worlds—celestial love going forth into combat/aid, then spiritual ordering into a cosmos, then natural effects established (“worlds above and below”).
2) Second day: enthronement and reign in a “new aeon/new earth”—the stabilized spiritual degree (ordered government, truths enthroned) in preparation for direct celestial unveiling.
3) Third day: the Father’s unveiling, inmost presence, renewal, and internal satiation—explicit celestial influx (love/mercy) filling the lower degrees and perfecting their forms.

The “two deaths” likewise follow correspondential logic: (a) a cosmic/macrocosmic death = mixture of light with darkness (confusion/impurity) until separation (purification), and (b) an individual/microcosmic death = deprivation of light/air/soul (loss of reception of higher influx) leading to binding in ultimates. The chapter’s numbers therefore read as states (three = completeness of a whole process; two = dual outcomes / two modes of separation), not as administrative taxonomy.

One local pressure point appears: “five sons of the First Man.” Here the number five does not function as a rival structural degree-taxonomy (no pentad of ‘fathers’ etc.), but as a sufficient multiplicity (powers/forces) within the first-day scene. It does not create positions 4–5 after a completed triad, and so does not bear the signature of Mani’s pentadic expansion. Another pressure point is a seam-marker phrase (“And also…”) inside the second death description; it reads as rhetorical stitching rather than structural addition and can be smoothed without altering meaning.

**Summary:** Findings: 4 total (2 confirmed-structure, 2 local seam/coherence issues). Changes made: 3 paragraphs lightly smoothed (¶9, ¶10, ¶15) without altering correspondential claims. Dominant result: the chapter’s core triadic and dual numeric architecture is genuine (three days / two deaths) with no pentadic expansion present. Confidence: high on overall structure; medium on the precise wording of smoothed lacuna-sutures in ¶9.

## Chapter 40: Concerning the Three Things that were established by the Light

**¶2** Three things would be fashioned by the Light through necessity. For there can be no other way nor other result, to allow them to stay without coming about. For the darkness, the enemy, must be bound by them; he who stood up against the Light from the beginning. Due to this, these things have no (other) result, to allow them to stay without coming about.

**¶3** Now, the first thing is the new luminous earth, which was set and built on top of this earth [and they placed the fetter beneath] it, namely that alien body [which they] built upon it.

**¶4** The second thing is this: They will [bring together the dark fire into it] and they [will shut it in within] the new earth, the coffin [and the prison] ⟨p.105⟩ of the dark fire, the companion of the enemy.

**¶5** The third thing is the souls of all the sinners who have been condemned by their deeds. They were [set to lamentation, and there is] over them woe; they have been condemned according to the wicked deeds they had committed, and due to this they will be crucified on the enemy, he who had raised himself up against the greatness.

**¶6** [*] They will seal up this final lump when all the likenesses and images of every shape will be nailed in it. Those will be bound by this last fetter for eternity, and be placed as a footstool and a mat for this coffin [of the new earth] which is the prison of the enemy. It will be placed, being a boundary-marker and a great void between the two, between the darkness and the enemy, so that they cannot reach one another, nor can they hear their voices; rather they will become sterile as they are separated from each other for ever and ever.

> **Structural findings:**
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶2-5 (the three items): The chapter announces and delivers a list of three established ‘things’ (new earth; confinement of dark fire; condemned souls). The numbering is stable and complete; no 4th/5th items intrude, and ther
> - [coherence_issue] ¶¶6 (imagery cluster: “footstool and a mat and a rag”): The triple metaphor (“footstool… mat… rag”) is rhetorically emphatic but risks sliding from functional correspondence into polemical degradation. It does not introduce a new taxonomy, but it does look
> - [seam_artifact] ¶¶6 (“Also,”): The connective ‘Also,’ reads like an editorial bridge rather than a required logical step.

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter describes a triadic work in ultimates (the natural degree) that necessarily follows spiritual combat: containment, confinement, and fixation. In correspondential terms, it is about how disordered will (the “enemy/darkness”) is prevented from further influx by being (1) given a fixed boundary in ultimates (“new earth” as an outer container), (2) having its active burning compulsion restricted (“dark fire” shut in), and (3) having the human adhesions to it (confirmed states of evil in “souls of sinners”) fixed in a final state. The structure is a genuine three: it is not an emanation of divine persons upward, but a completion-sequence downward into the ultimate plane—how heaven’s order secures a permanent separation so that the two loves (heavenly and infernal) no longer mingle. The tone is strongly carceral (“coffin/prison”), which may reflect Manichaean mythic framing; yet the underlying architecture—ultimate boundary, binding of active evil, and fixation of adherent states—is coherent with discrete degrees and influx (higher order terminating in a stable natural effect).

**Summary:** Findings: 3 (2 minor seam/amplification issues; 1 confirmed triadic structure). Changes made: removed one bridge connective (‘Also’) and reduced one rhetorical triple (‘footstool… mat… rag’) to functional supports to keep correspondence primary. No pentadic expansion detected; the chapter’s ‘three’ is structurally genuine. Confidence: high.

---

## Chapter 41: Concerning the Three Blows that befell the Enemy because of the Light.

**¶1** The Darkness itself, the Enemy, has received three bad blows with three wars; and worse dangers from the Light in those three wars.

**¶2** The first: (The enemy) was brought out to the middle and separated from his dark earth, whence he had departed. He was vanquished in that first war, caught and [bound] by the living soul.

**¶3** The second blow is the time when he will be dissolved and melted, and he perishes in this great fire. He is destroyed and overthrown, from the images that are all by which he exists. And he will be gathered in [to the] fetter corresponding to his archetype, and be made like he was from the first beginning.

**¶4** The third blow that will befall the Enemy is the 'rolling back' [at the] end when everything will be separated, and male will be divided [from] female. Now, the male will be bound in the lump, but the female [will be thrown] in to the tomb. For it will be divided [and be shut up, divided] stone in their midst, for generation upon generation, for ever and ever.

**¶5** ⟨p.106⟩ This is the way that the binding of the enemy will come about, in a fetter that is burdensome and strong, one from which there is never escape; because [they have achieved] his binding and bound him for ever. And they have achieved his being set apart, and he has been set apart for ever.

> **Structural findings:**
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶1–¶4: The chapter is natively triadic: it explicitly presents three blows/wars and maintains that structure consistently through the body without additional fourth/fifth positions.
> - [correspondential_observation] ¶¶2: “Brought out to the middle” and “separated from his dark earth” describes exposure and restriction of the Enemy’s proprium in an intermediate plane where it can be judged and bound.
> - [correspondential_observation] ¶¶3: The Enemy’s second defeat is the collapse of his ‘images’ under great fire, followed by confinement into a fetter matching his archetype—evil returned to its own form.
> - [correspondential_observation] ¶¶4: The ‘rolling back’ with separation of male/female portrays the end of generative conjunction in the opposite order: falsity and evil are prevented from recombining to produce further states.
> - [confirmed_structure] ¶¶5: Paragraph 5 functions as a closing reinforcement of the ‘fetter’ theme and does not introduce new numbered positions; it reads as a legitimate concluding emphasis rather than a pentadic overlay.

**Spiritual Assessment:** This chapter is a compact eschatology of the “Enemy” (Darkness) told as three successive defeats. Spiritually it describes the progressive curbing of the proprium (the selfhood that claims life as its own) under the descending influx of the Light: first restraint, then dissolution of falsity by truth’s fire, then final separation that ends illicit conjunction.

1) In the first war the Enemy is “brought out to the middle” and “separated from his dark earth.” In correspondences, being drawn into the “middle” signals exposure in a common plane where judgment can occur: what was hidden in one’s own ground (earth = the ultimate plane that supports a life) is forced into manifest form. The binding “by the living soul” indicates that life from the Divine (soul as living influx) restrains the active outbreak of evil; it is not yet annihilation but containment. This is the first mercy/judgment: evil is limited so that further ordering can proceed.

2) The second blow is dissolution in “great fire.” Fire here is in the opposite sense: not heavenly love warming and enlightening, but the consuming activity that destroys the Enemy’s forms. The text says he is overthrown “from the images… by which he exists,” which shows a key spiritual mechanism: evil persists by phantasms—fabricated appearances, persuasive falsities, and imagined identities. When these “images” are broken, the Enemy’s coherence collapses. Yet the outcome is not mere scattering: he is “gathered into [the] fetter corresponding to his archetype.” That is, evil is confined into its own form—returned to its own ruling love’s pattern (“archetype”) so it can no longer parasitize the Light’s order.

3) The third blow is the “rolling back” at the end: “everything will be separated, and male will be divided from female.” The male/female pair corresponds to understanding/will, truth/good, and (in the opposite sense) falsity/evil as they conjoin for production (“generation”). Here the point is not biology but the termination of generative conjunction: the Enemy’s capacity to propagate states is shut down by separation. Binding the “male… in the lump” and casting the “female… into the tomb” expresses a final immobilization: truth-forms (even falsified ‘male’) are compacted and made inert; the lust of producing (perverted ‘female’) is entombed—no more births of new evils. The “stone in their midst” corresponds to an ultimate barrier in the natural degree—hard, fixed truth as a boundary—so that the separation persists “generation upon generation.”

Overall, the chapter is less about mapping a triad of discrete degrees than about the staged process by which evil is first checked, then deprived of its persuasive appearances, and finally prevented from recombining to reproduce. The ruling love of the text is the protection of order: the Light’s governance works by limitation, exposure, and final separation, so that what is opposite cannot continue to draw life from what is good.

**Summary:** Findings: 5 (3 correspondential observations, 2 confirmed-structure confirmations). Changes made: 0. Dominant observation: the triadic sequence portrays a staged curbing of the proprium—restraint, dissolution of deceptive ‘images,’ and final separation that ends generative conjunction. Overall confidence: high; no indicators of pentadic expansion are present.

## Chapter 43: Concerning the Vessels.

**¶3** Then he bailed and scooped them from this earth in these three garments, they of wind and of water and of fire, which swathed his body. **¶4** The element that is left in water is the pungency and the salinity of this salty inner sea that surrounds the worlds.

**¶5** And the remnant that is left from the darkness is this black mountain that exists in the surroundings of the sunrise. For when the sun would shine over the world no sunlight is visible there, because the darkness that gushes from it manifests above it.

**¶6** And the remnant that remains behind from the fire, upon the earth, are all the mountains and islands from where fumes of fire gush up and are visible. For these things are the remnant of the great vessel, the vestige that was conjoined with this fire that comes up from the earth; together with the mountains and hills [ ... ] places where no fruit sprouts on the trees [ ... ] greater than that place from the limb and [ ... / ... ] the trees shall not bear fruit in that place.

**¶8** Happen you know this! When the Father of Life discharged these vessels from above upon the earth, he poured them out for this reason: so that also the water and the darkness and the fire that exist in these three earths would be drawn and ascend towards they that had come down from above, and the earths would be purified and cleansed. Due to this he poured these vessels down to the earth, of water and of darkness and of fire, so that also the water and darkness and fire that exist in the earths would be drawn up towards they that had descended from above.

**¶9** Now, first of all, he discharged the water, the waste, the poison of the rulers who are above. He cleansed the firmaments of the [water ... ] he poured down the dark waters. [Just as the] waters fell from heaven to earth, so also the waters below in the earths were drawn and came up from below towards the waters that had come from above to the earths. The waters [were] purified of the dark poison that exists in them. Then he swept them outside and made them a great vessel and a wall of sea that surrounds the universe, and on which the ships sail.

**¶10** After the water, he discharged the darkness. He purified the firmaments and dried from them the darkness with which they are weighed down. He discharged it down to the earth. Just as he had purified the firmaments, so also he purified the earths. The darkness that is in the earths was drawn up towards the darkness that had been discharged from above. He swept them both out and shut them in that [ ... ] of darkness [ ... / ... ] the water.

**¶11** And he discharged the fire from heaven to earth, [ ... at] that time again the fire that was shut [in] the earth below was drawn and came up towards its kind. Fire was added to fire. Afterwards, he swept it out and cast it into the outer underworld, which [he had constructed for] them outside the universe.

**¶12** In the same way that he purified the firmaments above as a place for the light-givers to journey [ ... / ... ] so also he purified the earths below and swept from them the fire and the darkness and the water [ ... ] He has purified them for the ascent of these three powers, they of the wind and the water and the fire, which ascend before the Porter and traverse the earths.

## Chapter 44: Concerning the Sea Giant

**¶2** After the Living Spirit constructed things and the worlds, he came up and set himself upon this earth. ## Chapter 47: Concerning the Four great Things

**¶1** The wheel of the stars, which is displayed to you, is a great mighty thing! Also, it is one among the four great things; since the whole ruling-power is assigned to four parts.

**¶2** The first part is the powers who occur in the ten firmaments that exist above the wheel, and the ruling-power that is bound in them, as it is imprisoned within them in the heavens that are above.

**¶3** The second part is the eight earths that are below; the four that are composite, and the four places of darkness. The second part extends from this earth, upon which mankind walks, towards the underside.

**¶4** The third part is the walls and the vessels that surround the worlds. They are the four mountains and the three vessels; with what is in them, and the ruling-power that is trapped within them, and the powers who are masters over them.

**¶5** The fourth part is the sphere of the stars that is appointed with great diligence, as it is ordered in a [ ... m]any [ ... ]; that of the Father of Life, [and that of the Mother of] the living.

**¶6** ⟨p.119⟩ Its [ ... ] very [ ... ] with diligence, because of the great and cruel powers, and the titanic figures, [who are] in it; they being suspended from it. So, all the first-born and the leaders of all the rulers, they who were existing in the three parts, these were garnered. They were collected together, seized, bound and assigned on this wheel. Each was apportioned by these three parts. They were distributed, entwined, and weighted against their companions of the same size.

**¶7** Still, for its part, the wheel is equal to them in its strength; the powers of the enmity who are bound on it. Indeed, they were suspended on the circling wheel because of their cruelty; so that, due to the wind and the motion in which they go round, they comprehend not the place wherein they are set. For if they had been bound in the heavens, the heavens would not have had the strength to support them. Again, if they had been assigned on the earths, on the fixtures that are below, they also would not have had the strength to support them. Rather, they were suspended from the wheel of the circling stars; so that the power and the light that should come up from below can be received, and given to the heavenly ones above them. Conversely, whatever will fall from above, whether cold or hot, the wheel of the sphere will also take it and cast it out upon the entire earth, upon all the wood.

**¶8** Behold, the ordinance of the wheel is in this form. For the powers who are entwined on the wheel are like this: a king mastering his kingdom by this mighty legionary force of all the leaders of the kingdom; and with all the armour of the soldiery that is collected together for him; and also with the mass of the property of the kingdom; and the beautiful lustful aspect of the women who are collected together for him; together with [ ... ] property [ ... ] and those cattle [ ... ] that have been distributed about among his retinue. Yet, if you ask about that king, he is matched with all his retinue. His retinue, for its part, is matched against his entire kingdom. Judgement shall come forth from him, vindication and condemnation, to his entire kingdom; by his wisdom, skill and strength.

**¶9** Now, just as for this king, who is matched against the measure of his entire kingdom by his wisdom, skill and strength; so also is it for the powers who are bound on the sphere as compared to the ruling-power, and the powers who exist in the entire zone. Like the king towards his retinue; so too is it that this totality is gathered to the sphere, to seven leaders, to twelve signs of the zodiac. As all the cities are gathered to the king, again they gather to the leaders of the universe. All that is done in the universe above and below: the battles and the disorders and the captivity and the hunger and the lust and the property; so shall they increase and diminish through these leaders. They are the motivaters of the whole created order; the totality being gathered in to them the way that I have told you. I have opened your eyes!

---

## Chapter 48: Concerning the Conduits.

**¶1** There are three conduits that exist through the entire zone, from the abyss to its heights.

**¶2** The first conduit is the root of all the powers above, they that exist in all the heavens. They are cast off and fettered on the earths below; because the earth below is the ‘stripping off’, the garment and the body of the powers that are above the heavens.

**¶3** For when the Father of Life constructed the heavenly worlds, he divested them of their bodies, [he] threw them to the earths below.

**¶4** So, the upper worlds are of the soul and the spirit; but the worlds below are of the body and the carcass [...] ⟨p.121⟩ Now as for this, he [...] the powers of heaven; he sealed it on their bodies and their carcasses that are on the earths, so that when life comes up from the fullness of these earths, it can be entirely drawn up to the heights in the roots of what is heavenly, that are fastened in their bodies. And also, all life can be purified in that place.

**¶5** However, the waste that will be swept from the purified one who ascends to the heights will be let fall to the ground through the conduits of these conduits, and will be poured down [...] and thrown to the underside.

**¶6** This is the first conduit, which comes from the powers of the heavens to their bodies and their carcasses that are upon the earth; also, from their bodies and [their] carcasses that are on the earths to the powers of the heavens.

**¶7** The second conduit is the one that comes from the temples and dwellings and cities that exist in the heavens down to the ground, to the five shapes of tree that grow upon the earth. The life comes up from the trees to the temples and cities. Life too, and the residue of the heavenly ones, comes down to the trees in a conduit.

**¶8** And the third conduit is all the powers and householders who dwell in all the firmaments, whose roots come down from them to the five worlds of flesh that creep upon the earth, being fixed to one another. Now, the power and the life that shall be gathered in from the worlds of flesh are dispersed among them in various shapes. The powers above shall draw them to the upper side through conduits.

**¶9** [The was]te too, and the lust and the evil-doing and the anger that will be greater in the powers of [heave]n, shall be poured to the ground through their various conduits. They shall be discharged upon mankind and the other remaining animals. When what is heavenly will wash the waste and the stench and the poison down on the creations of the flesh below, in their turn the creations shall be greater in lust and anger and evil-doing against each other through the action of their fathers who are on high.

**¶10** Still, look at the wheel of the stars! It has no [root in this earth], but its roots are bound in the totality. The wheel of stars, then, receives life through the power and the life that ascend from the earths through conduits and the firmaments above. It (also) takes life from those conduits that receive up to the firmaments and the heavenly worlds above it, it takes life out from that place.

**¶11** Look, in that the wheel has no root in this earth, from where did the stars and signs of the zodiac find this authority? They became masters over these five fleshes and five trees. Even though you have told us that the wheel has no root there! For you said to us that every thing, if it can be reduced from the earth, the heavenly powers above draw it through a conduit. Also, we see that every thing that a person will attain, whether it be wealth that comes into his possession or poverty that will accrue to him, or his sickness and health; he attains it through the signs of the zodiac and the star under which he shall be born.

**¶12** Nevertheless, what I proclaimed to you, namely that the wheel has no root therein, I have recounted to you properly. It is set aright in its position. For when the Living Spirit, the Father of Life, constructed the heavens and the firmaments above, he bound their roots into the earths below; in their carcasses that he had stripped from them. Yet, when he constructed the wheel, [he] did not bind its root into the earths, but he bound the root of the wheel by the sea giant.

**¶13** And when [the] Ambassador came, he displayed his image and purified and brought the light out from all [the] heavens. Matter, however, which is present in all the rulers, spurted out against the image of the Ambassador. It went up in order to reach it, but reached it not. It dropped back and fell to the [under]side. Now, when it had fallen from above, it separated out into three parts.

**¶14** The first part fell to the wheel; another part fell to the earth; an[other part fell to the se]a. ⟨p.123⟩ [By means] of the part that fell to the sphere, [the r]oot was bound in the trees and flesh. Therefore, because of this, they received authority and became masters in the five worlds of the flesh and the five worlds of the tree.

**¶15** Through the nature that came down [upo]n the wheel the root was bound in the trees. Also, by means of the abortions that came to the ground, another root was bound in the fleshes.

**¶16** Therefore, because of this, the stars and signs of the zodiac received authority over the trees and the fleshes: through the root of Sin that fell down; and [the ro]ot of the abortions that were aborted there, they fell to the ground.

**¶17** However, the[se] other conduits that [the] Living Spirit has bound in the e[a]rths, they that the soul of the elements comes up through to the heavens; those (conduits) are not bound to the wheel. Rather, they are plundered from them by the powers on the wheel, that hang from it.

**¶18** Just as you have instructed us and put together this lesson on [...] you have told us about the prototype of the conduits. The first is the one that comes from the firmaments to the earths, and the one that comes from the wheel to the sea giant. That is only one. So, the other root is the one that comes [fr]om [the temples] to the trees. The third is bound by means of the abortions in the f[leshes]; also in the fleshes the fathers of the abortions who are in the heavens [...] are bound and twined, and the [y] that [...]

**¶20** You have inquired about a [...] and you have asked about this lesson in superiority, great [...] the earth from which much life ascends [...] the Father of Life has drawn them. ⟨p.124⟩ He has fastened them from the powers [...] he has bound them in the earths. (These conduits) are the roots of the ent[ire] ruling-power that the life of the living soul is drawn up through, it coming up from the whole earth, from all the mountains, fields and islands.

**¶21** After that first great root is the root of the trees, which is bound to the temples and the buildings above. For there also is much great life coming up from the tree and the entire Cross, being drawn up from them to the upper side like a great host.

**¶22** What, however, is lesser [...] of their conduit [...] he takes up [...] the roots [...] the earths, they [...] the whole earth in all the worlds. Again, these of the trees are present in the whole earth, whether small or great; but the root of mankind [...] is not present in the whole earth, other than only this world of the south. Therefore, even now these fleshes do not make [...] in the entire world of the south [...] there is a small part of them in [...] of the upper side of the world of the south in [...] in number than the [...]

**¶23** I have instructed you about the roots [...] them, [I] have taught you that they are bound by their [roots ...] with one another. They stir one another, they draw life from one another through the action of the powers above, which stirs the nature that dwells in them all.

**¶24** Now, when the Matter that is in them will be crushed and despoiled, then that Matter too shall [...] its members and overturn them and [...] each other [...] stir [...] ⟨p.125⟩

## Chapter 49: Concerning the Wheel and the Conduits

**¶2** The reason that the conduits shall not be cut, for they are not cut, is because they are spiritual. Thus the conduits are like the waters through which ships sail. You shall find the prow that is in position in the front part of the ship divides the waters, casting them back and forth. So, when it parts the waters and that ship opens up the water with its cleaving prow, immediately and without delay the waters mingle with each other again behind the ship; and the path of the ship shall not be discernible in the midst of the waters.

**¶3** Just as when a bird flies up in the air, for its path is not visible in the air, and in the way of mankind, in the sun within the conduits. They also shall not be tangled up in them, and neither shall you find their path through the air known.

**¶4** This is also the case with the wheel. It turns through the conduits and the air. The wheel shall not cut the conduits, nor are the conduits themselves entangled in the wheel.

---

## Chapter 50: Concerning these Names: God, Rich One, and Angel; who they are.

**¶3** There are these three archetypes [in the land of light, and there is no measure to] them!

**¶4** So, the ones who are called 'god', are the gods whom the Father has summoned from himself. He has established them after the likeness of his greatness.

**¶5** Conversely, these who are named 'rich [ ... ]', are the evocations of the first rich gods of the Father; because, when the Father had summoned (the first), they themselves summoned evocations. They called them 'rich'.

**¶6** They too, the rich ones, have summoned their evocations. They call them 'angel'. These are the three archetypes who occur in the land of light. There is no measure to apply to them!

**¶7** Once again, listen! Other persons who are named 'god' are the emanations who have come from the Father; the evocations of the Father whom he summoned forth. They came out to the contest and humiliated the enmity.

**¶8** Conversely, the 'rich ones' are the evocations of these first living words. They too, the rich ones, have come and performed and fulfilled the pleasure of the greatness, in the worlds that are above and below.

**¶10** Again, they are called 'god', all the gods who belong to the household of the Great Spirit.

**¶11** Conversely, the ones who are called 'angel', are all the angels who belong to the household of the Beloved of the Lights.

**¶12** The ones who are called 'rich', are all the rich ones who belong to the household of the glorious Ambassador.

---

**¶2** When the [First] Man came forth against the ene[my ...] ⟨p.127⟩ garment [...] the darkness with the garment of [of] living fire. With that garment he withered the tumescence (?) of the enemy; he brought down i[t]s foolery and its vanity.

**¶3** Now, this one alone was sufficient for them. [Y]et, si[n]ce the living fire was weakened, because of this he added [to it another fou]r garments: that of the wind, that of the water, that of the light, and that of the a[i]r. And this, (why) has he given the four? He gave them s[o] th[a]t they would be helpe[r]s to t[h]at god, wh[ich i]s the living fire; so that [...] not with [in] the living f[ire], and penetrate it; and [the po]ison and the [enmi]ty cut it off.

**¶4** While the fire received a bl[ow and] a wound in its body; yet still, [...] all the powers of da[rk]ness because of it [...] It [...] necessity [...] brothers, to cause them to receive a blow and a woun[d to]o. The power of the en/emy mixed in with them. Rath[er, ex]cept [the air]. The darkness did not mix in with it, nor was the burning of the enemy able to penet/rate it.

**¶5** Never-theless, even the one that remained behind without the wound, which is the air, it stripped off garment and power. It gave it to its [fo]ur brothers in its place; so that by that [ga]rment of the living air they would become strong, and [...] profit, and endure, namely its other four brothers. And it [be]came for them [...] life.

## Chapter 52: Concerning the [ ... ] of the Light

**¶2** [ ... ] how far [ ... the lig]ht that came forth from [ ... ] in that place in a [ ... ] alone [ ... ] ⟨p.128⟩ the greatness of the light with one another for ever, and nor did one of them transgress the command and order that his brother had entrusted to him forever.

**¶3** However, the darkness, the enemy, was doomed and overcome; because of the arising of disharmony it was divided within itself. Even the kingdom was divided within itself; [and] ev[en] the [powers, who had] left their abyss behind and come up fo[llow-ing] it, were doomed because of the disunity that was present in all of them. The darkness was destroyed, and its other powers were humiliated, because the p[ower] of that first light functions in the same way as the kingdom of this world.

**¶4** As long as its [associates in the m]ystery are in agreement with it, th[at] kingdom [ ... ] and they marvel at it and it receives glory, the one that [ ... ] and it surpasses and is even more exalted than this other kingdom, which is about [to burst out] against [it].

**¶5** Still, the kingdom, [the one that] its associates in the mystery [are in agreement] upon, is compelled to condemn that (other) kingdom; and the[y] are humiliated and come under the hand of this kingdom that is set up against it.

## Chapter 53: Concerning the First Man.

**¶1** At the time when the First Man was sent to the war in the first garment of [living fire], which he gave to the first nature that exists [...] in that single garment. [...] he hunted Matter, the thought of death, [...] and all its other powers.

**¶2** ⟨p.130⟩ When the First Man came up from the abyss, [...] the Living Spirit brought the rulers whom he had hunted [...] element, that [...] of [the land of] darkness, that he might construct from them this universe and fashion everything appropriate for the greatness. From that time on, the place of the storehouses of the elements [...] he destroyed and laid waste the [...] all that were in it.

**¶3** Just like a marsh, of which the fish and birds are snared in a net, and only the waters are left behind and the reeds and the plants that grow in it, this is also the case with the storehouses of death. The [First] Man snared their counsels and [their k]in that were [in them], and the storehouses were left behind in their [dwelli]ng place.

**¶4** So then, the case of the land of darkness is like this, as is that of the storehouses that were in it.

**¶5** Just as the body of man has many limbs in it, [the land of darkness] has [storehouses from] its beginning [...] all [the limbs] that hang from the great head of the [body]. A single root is the root of them all, because they all hang from the great head that is on top of the body. Should a limb be cut from the tip of [the]se small [h]eads, the great head that is on top of them [al]l stays in its place the way that it is [...] you find this person alive. Al[l] these sm[all h]eads are firmly set in it [...]

**¶6** Wh[en] they will cut from them [...] all of them, these little [heads] shall [...] the corpses, because they might see [...] be firmly set [...] in the body, which they joined wi[th it ...]

**¶7** In exactly this way, it is also the case that the storehouses of darkness are the same, the things from which the [darkness gushed] forth.

## Chapter 54: [Concerning] the Quality of the Garments

**¶1** At the time when the F[ir]st [Man ...] he gave the leadership to his five [sons ... the] Living Spirit and the Mother of Life [...] as the entire ruling-power is entwined and crucified [...] they were poured out and mixed with each other, the light with the darkness and the darkness with the light.

**¶2** At that time the Living Spirit called one of his sons, one rich with power. He gave to him the leadership of the garments. Now, he himself [...] of [...] he hung them and set them up [...] of the Father of Life [...] he set up all things [...] he opened [...] ten firmaments [...] the wheel of the stars [...] them and the [...].

**¶3** ⟨p.132⟩ The Living Spirit is like this: Just as [...] and an architect who might build a dwelling for the king, and divide it up into separate houses and [separate ...] everything that is required for beauty [...] and he cleans them. And also the stones [...] them for beauty and {loveliness}, and he arranges them and makes them so that should the king come and inhabit that palace, he might glorify him.

**¶4** Furthermore, the Father of Life resembles a wise and skilled carpenter [...] before it was worked [...] before it was established and made beautiful and [...] carpenter [...] works in it, but [...] in him in his [...] in his understanding, and he brings the saw and the adze, and cuts it into separate pieces [...] it and he pulls the [...] it with the {plane} and he smooths it. He shall make many [...] wooden implements, arranging them shape by shape; he cuts and produces wooden implements from it, wooden implements of different kinds, (such as) dishes, bowls. This wood, that [...] since previously it had not been worked before it was sealed by [the ski]ll and wisdom of this dextrous carpenter. He cut it up into different sorts of implements useful to his master.

**¶5** So is it also with the Father of Life. In his wisdom he has [...] in limbs [...] palace [...] in it or in a [...] useful [...] the ships of light [...] all the other works [...] in the [...] so that when the great king comes, he who is the Third Ambassador, who [...] he might dwell in this universe [...]

**¶6** ⟨p.133⟩ ... live. The light, however, that is in it [...] wise, and he takes it up to the land; but the waste and the darkness will be gathered and thrown down to the abyss.

## Chapter 55: Concerning the Fashioning of Adam

**¶1** There are some amo]ng us telling us that we [know (?) ... ] that the (Ambassador) consents about [tha]t f[igure] of flesh; he who shall be fashi[oned ... ] at another time. And next, he says: [The Ambassa]dor displayed his image in the un[iverse]. The rulers and the powers of the universe saw h[is image], and they formed their shapes after his likeness, who are Adam and Eve. Behold, you see that god con[sen]ts to its happening; because of this, he has displayed his image to the rulers; and they constructed [ ... ... ] If he was not consenting to humankind, he would not have uncovered his image, nor they fashioned mankind after his likeness!

**¶2** Regarding the Ambassador, the occasion when he came displaying his image, he [d]id not come so th[a]t he might reveal his image to the ru[l]ers in the universe. Rather, he came and displayed to the universe be[cause of h]is soul and his son, who is [ ... ] every [ ... ], that he might enable him to live [ ... ] and make for him a release; and to free (the soul) from every bond and all the fetters, in which it is entangled and bound.

**¶3** Still, you, look and see! The rulers and the authorities, in that they had no form in [ ... ] him, they lusted for his image. They reflected upon themselves, that [they] had no [one] like him in their creation. They sealed hi[s im]a[g]e in their heart, within their soul. Afterward[s, they] built it according to his likeness; they formed Adam and [Ev]e. They sealed the image of the exalted one in their [soul]. So, they [co]pied with an imitation; but they have not [copied i]n truth! Rather, just as [ ... ... ] it is made as these, for [i]t came [ ... ] but it is not l[ik]e him in truth. [Again, so too] is this figure of ma[nkind. They copi]ed it against the image of the exalted one [ ... ]

**¶4** Similarly the [t]ree, for while it is like the [ ... it is] not [copi]ed in truth.

**¶5** This matt[er is comparable to] this: As a great, fre[e] woman [ ... ] within her [ ... ... ] rich in wealth [ ... ] po[ss]essions; she is also beautiful in her beauty, and is virtuous in her modesty. She walks in a free and reputable position. The fame of her beauty has spread in every c[i]ty. She sur[passes ... ] Behold h[er] chamber [ ... ] one who is mad, because [of] the nobles and the great ones. Al[so], they lust for her beautiful face. They did not [ ... ] her, because she is hidden in her palace [ ... ] within in a [ ... ] meet that woman [ ... ] his so[n]; or rather the head [ ... t]hat garment. This free woman shall [ ... ] leave the cha[mber] behind her and come to the street [ ... ] her head [ ... ] and she reveals her [face and her beauty ... ] because of her beloved broth[er]. This wom[an], on whom no man ever looked, nor did they ever see her fa[ce], as she neither desires nor rejoices [ ... ... ] leave her chamber behind her, and come in the midst of mankind [ ... ] and everyone view her. The hones[t] men and the nobles, even the servants too, and the [ ... ] look at her.

**¶6** ⟨p.135⟩ In accord with this simile: Again, [this] too [is] how [ ... ] the Ambassador becomes [ ... ... ] as this free woman [ ... not] according to wantonness, in pride, nor [ ... she co]nsented to the outer world, she [ ... ... ], she revealed her face and her [beau]ty [ ... ] but only due to the constraint of grief [about] her [bel]ov[ed one].

**¶7** Now, this too is the way of the Third Ambassador, when he came and display[ed his] image with a show towards all the powers. [He did] not [co]me so that all the rulers and the author[it]ies would sculpt a form [after h]is likeness. R[ather], he came because of his son being crucified in the totality; [fo]r he would release him, free him, and rescue him from oppres-sion. Yet, when the rulers looked o[n] him, [th]ey lusted for his image. They fashioned after his [like]ness Adam and Eve, without the consent of the gre[at]ness.

**¶9** [Understa]nd also this other (matter) that I will teach you. For, [a]s with Adam and Eve, whom the rulers fashioned and [se]aled after the image of the exalted one, so too this [i]s how a manifold divinity and many powers and angels [ ... received] the stamp of that image, which was displayed to them. For, in the majesty of the l[ig]ht and (the image's) love, they glowed with their desire and they were undone. Th[ey] were eager for their lust; like a m[an who looks] to the majesty of a lusted after face, and [ ... ] and he is eager for the {} of the desire.

**¶10** ⟨p.136⟩ This is [als]o the case with those powers, when they looked on that majestic and beautiful [imag]e, they () [ ... ] a first image. They received the stamp of th[at image], which was displayed to them. They copied [ ... wh]ich th[ey co]pied out in a likeness, it being made [ ... ... ] the ones that they had put forth, which had received a [stamp of this im]age; but inward even to the rocks [ ... ... ] which occur from place to place. The {} of that image, which was displayed in the universe [ ... ] they received a stamp, it being revealed there, and an impress.

**¶11** Now, this new birth that they begot in a new [ ... ] in the world, is Adam and Eve. [There is not a] form in their world since the beginning like it; because that [im]age [occ]urs not in the worlds b[el]ow.

**¶12** Not all the figures that have been fashioned were fashioned by birth from displayed intercourse. Rather, there are other figures fashioned [ ... ]

**¶13** One of them is the sea [gi]ant, the 'sweeping' from the sphere, which the [Father] of Life discharged into the sea. Its own fire and thought fashioned it [in] a figure.

**¶14** The second figure is the one [that] fell to the earth. It made the sea its womb. Its own l[us]t became its sculptor. It alone fashioned it to a nature, which is the root of death. Yet, when it came up from the sea, it [ ... ] that it might destroy the works [ ... ] of Life. They immediately sent against it the Adamant of Light, the great 'instruction of vigour'. He humbled it in the districts of the north. He has trampled underfoot and set h[im]self upon it, until the end of the world.

**¶15** ⟨p.137⟩ [The] third figure is the nature that fell on wh[at] is dr[y. It] fashioned the tree and set itself there. [ ... the] new figure that was fashioned [ ... d]isplays.

**¶16** So, in accord with the essence they [ ... the dark]ness; but yet th[ey] did not come with the King of Dark[ness to the wo]rld. Rather, their fashioning [came] about [ ... ] they are composite and have been displayed in the world.
